That is, unless $\mathrm P=\mathrm{NP}$, no polynomial-time algorithm can in general find optimal protocols using fewer than $2^{CC_α(G) -2}$ bits.
  We note that our results strictly weaken the assumptions required by prior work in the multi-agent information aggregation literature, filling a gap that had remained elusive even for games with constant $CC_α(G)$. In particular, prior guarantees for agreement-based information aggregation rely on structural assumptions such as informational substitutes or weak learnability. We show that these assumptions already imply $CC_α(G) = O(1)$ and are therefore more restrictive conditions than required by our protocol to succeed.
  On a technical level, our results involve a novel strengthening of the Frieze-Kannan weak regularity lemma and yield the following powerful polynomial-time transformation tool: for every communication game $G$, it constructs a game $\hat G$ that is a coarsening of the agents' observation spaces into constant-size partitions, such that $G$ and $\hat G$ are indistinguishable with respect to every short communication protocol. This coarsening theorem is the engine behind our algorithm and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [171] [A Polynomial-Time Rule Satisfying Full Justified Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05397)
*Fabian Frank,Jannik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种满足完全正当代表（FJR）的贪心合理性候选人规则的变体，该变体可在多项式时间内计算，解答了FJR委员会能否高效计算的开放问题；同时否定了MES规则返回的委员会总能扩展以满足FJR的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多赢家投票中，比例代表是一个核心目标，而FJR是实现该目标的一种强概念。然而此前是否存在多项式时间算法以计算满足FJR的委员会是一个悬而未决的问题。此外，MES（Method of Equal Shares）返回的委员会是否总可扩展以满足FJR也是未解决的问题。

Method: 提出了Greedy Justified Candidate规则的一个变体（variant），证明该变体满足FJR，并能用多项式时间算法计算。通过构造反例，对MES委员会能否扩展至满足FJR的问题给出了否定答案。

Result: 1) 所提出的贪心规则变体满足FJR且可多项式时间求解；2) 证明了MES返回的委员会并非总能以保持FJR的方式被扩展，否定了该开放猜想。

Conclusion: 首次证明了满足FJR的委员会可在多项式时间内计算，并澄清了MES规则在FJR扩展性上的限制为进一步研究比例代表规则的理论性质提供了新方向。

Abstract: In approval-based multiwinner voting, voters submit an approval ballot on which basis a committee of fixed size $k$ has to be selected. Compared to single-winner voting this makes it possible to represent minorities in the selection of the committee as well. Thus, one of the most desirable goals of multiwinner voting is to satisfy proportional representation. In this paper we propose a variant of the Greedy Justified Candidate rule that satisfy the notion of Full Justified Representation (FJR). This variant can be computed in polynomial time answering the open question of whether an FJR committee can always be computed efficiently. Additionally, we answer an open question whether committees returned by MES can always be extended in a way to satisfy FJR negatively.

</details>


### [172] [Full Justified Representation under Hare and Droop Quotas in Polynomial Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05417)
*Yizhou Ai*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了审批制多赢家选举中完全正当代表（FJR）在Hare和Droop两种配额约定下的性质，提出了一种降预算算法，通过设定不同的候选人价格（λ_H=n/k 和 λ_D=n/(k+1)）分别返回满足Hare-FJR和更严格的Droop-FJR的委员会。两种保证共享历史支付不变量和终局行列记账论证，Droop证明需要新的剩余预算论证。算法在固定选民用例和候选人顺序下是确定性的，复杂度为O(kmn)次有理运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在审批制多赢家选举中，代表性公理（如完全正当代表FJR）是评价选举公平性的关键。此前Hare-FJR已有构造性证明，但更强的Droop-FJR公理缺乏有效的算法实现。本文旨在设计一种统一框架下的确定性算法，同时为Hare和Droop两种配额约定下的FJR提供构造性证明。

Method: 提出一种降预算算法：选民将其剩余预算分配到当前的代表性差距上，当针对某候选人的出价总和达到统一价格时该候选人被"购买"。通过设置两种候选人价格（λ_H=n/k对应Hare配额，λ_D=n/(k+1)对应Droop配额），算法分别实现两种FJR保证。证明框架包含历史支付不变量和终局行列记账论证，Droop情形额外需要剩余预算论证处理所有k个席位已满的情况。算法是确定性的，复杂度为O(kmn)次有理运算（k为席位、m为候选人数、n为选民数）。

Result: 该算法在Hare配额下返回满足Hare-FJR的委员会，在Droop配额下返回满足更强Droop-FJR公理的委员会。两种变体均保证确定性，且计算复杂度为O(kmn)次有理运算，为两种配额下的FJR提供了统一的构造性算法实现。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一的降预算算法框架，同时解决了Hare和Droop配额下FJR的构造性问题。Droop情形需要的额外剩余预算论证展示了两种配额证明间的细微差别。该算法不仅理论上有保证，其确定性特点和多项式时间复杂度也使其具有实际可用性。

Abstract: I study Full Justified Representation (FJR) in approval-based multiwinner elections under both the Hare and Droop quota conventions. I introduce a descending-budget algorithm in which voters distribute their remaining budgets across their current representation gaps and candidates are purchased whenever the resulting offers cover a common price. With candidate price $λ_H=n/k$, the algorithm returns a Hare-FJR committee; with candidate price $λ_D=n/(k+1)$, it returns a committee satisfying the more demanding Droop-FJR axiom of Casey and Elkind. The two guarantees share a historical-payment invariant and a terminal row--column accounting argument, while the Droop proof requires a new residual-budget argument when all $k$ paid seats are filled. Both variants are deterministic once the voter and candidate orders are fixed and use $O(kmn)$ rational operations.

</details>


### [173] [AI-Farol: Co-Evolutionary Dynamics in a Multi-Agent Two-Sided Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05479)
*Iosif Polenakis,Kalliopi Kastampolidou,Theodore Andronikos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 该论文将El Farol Bar博弈从静态协调模型重构为双面学习系统：酒吧从被动容量约束转变为具有AI学习能力的战略性机制设计者，同时引入部分可观测性，使代理与酒吧通过双向学习共同演化，为拥堵管理、资源分配和机制设计提供新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统El Farol Bar博弈将场所视为被动约束，忽略了机构在协调过程中的主动作用，且假设完全信息环境不够现实。作者旨在通过将酒吧建模为主动策略设计者并引入部分可观测性，更真实地刻画真实世界中协调与机构干预的耦合关系。

Method: 论文提出两个方向的扩展：(1)引入部分可观测性，代理仅能观察到部分历史出席者子集；(2)将酒吧从被动容量阈值转变为主动机制设计者，通过策略学习优化动态定价以平衡收入、利用率和可持续性约束。代理使用基于AI的学习算法在信息不完全下形成信念并调整出席策略，酒吧则应用策略学习方法进行动态定价优化，形成双面学习系统。

Result: 该框架将协调问题建模为有界理性代理与自适应机构之间的共演化过程，揭示了代理学习与机构策略调整之间的动态耦合关系，为理解复杂自适应系统中的协调机制提供了新的分析视角。

Conclusion: 通过将被动约束转化为主动设计者并加入部分可观测性，论文展示了AI驱动的双面学习如何重新定义协调问题，其结论对拥堵管理、资源分配以及机制设计等领域具有广泛的启示意义。

Abstract: The El Farol Bar game is a classical model of coordination under uncertainty that traditionally treats the venue as a passive constraint. In this work, we reconceptualize the problem by modeling the bar as a strategic player endowed with AI-driven learning capabilities. We extend the original framework in two principal directions: first, by introducing partial observability, whereby agents observe only subsets of past attendees; and second, by transforming the bar from a passive capacity threshold into an active mechanism designer that adjusts pricing policies to balance revenue, utilization, and sustainability constraints. Agents employ AI-based learning to form beliefs and adapt attendance strategies under incomplete information, while the bar applies policy learning to optimize dynamic pricing. The resulting two-sided learning system frames coordination as a co-evolutionary process between boundedly rational agents and an adaptive institution, offering insights into congestion management, resource allocation, and mechanism design in complex adaptive systems.

</details>


### [174] [Candidate Resignation Monotonicity in Approval-Based Committee Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06156)
*Yeeseok Oh,Dominik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究批准型委员会选举（ABC选举）中当选成员辞职场景下的"辞职单调性"性质，发现许多主流ABC规则不满足该性质，并提出最大流基础的最大支付规则（MPR）来同时满足表示公理和辞职单调性（的放宽形式），同时分析了相关策略操纵问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的选举委员会成员可能会在当选后辞职，需要重新运行投票规则来填补空缺。此时一个自然的期望是：仍然留在委员会中的原当选者应继续被选中。然而这一直觉性质尚未被系统研究，且需要确认它与已有表示公理（如JR、PJR+）的兼容性。

Method: 首先形式化定义辞职单调性（resignation monotonicity），并检验多个主流ABC规则是否满足该性质。通过将本题与动态候选集选举的既有研究建立联系，证明该性质与JR公理不相容。随后提出基于委员会选举网络表示中最大流的规则，设计整数版本的Maximum Payment Rule（MPR），证明其满足PJR+和放宽形式的辞职单调性，并研究其计算复杂度及策略免疫性。

Result: 众多主流ABC规则不满足辞职单调性；JR公理与辞职单调性在数学上不相容；基于最大流的规则可同时满足表示公理与辞职单调性；MPR满足PJR+和放宽形式的辞职单调性，虽然在一般情况下NP-hard但结构化领域中可计算；所有辞职单调规则及许多序贯规则对"引入弱候选者"的操纵免疫，但PAV规则可被这种策略操纵。

Conclusion: 辞职单调性是ABC选举中值得重视的性质，但传统规则很难同时满足它与表示公理。通过最大流框架设计的MPR规则为此提供了可行的折中方案，同时揭露了PAV等规则在候选人策略操纵方面的脆弱性，为后续设计鲁棒的委员会选举规则指明了方向。

Abstract: Approval-based committee (ABC) elections select a fixed-size committee of candidates based on voters' approval preferences. We study a setting where elected members may resign and formalize resignation monotonicity: when we re-run the voting rule after resignations, a resignation monotone rule must still elect all remaining previously winning candidates. We show that many prominent ABC rules fail this property, and by connecting our notion to prior work on ABC elections with dynamic candidate sets, we show that the justified representation (JR) axiom is incompatible with resignation monotonicity. It turns out that fractional committee voting does not suffer from this issue: rules based on using maximum flows in the network representation of an election instance can satisfy both representation axioms and resignation monotonicity. We design an integral version of maximum flow, the Maximum Payment Rule (MPR), and show that it satisfies a relaxed form of resignation monotonicity that only requires there to be some way of replacing the resigning candidates in a way that guarantees the PJR+ axiom. MPR is NP-hard to compute in general, but becomes tractable in structured domains. Finally, we study a strategic setting where a losing candidate may introduce new weak candidates to the election to try to become a winner. We show that all resignation monotone rules as well as many sequential rules are immune, but that the PAV rule can be manipulated in this way.

</details>


### [175] [Fair and Efficient Balanced Allocations for Additive Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06325)
*Benjamin Cookson,Nisarg Shah,Paritosh Verma*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在均衡约束（任何两个智能体所得商品数之差至多为一）下，不可分商品的公平且高效分配的可行性。主要结果证明了任意可加估值下，存在满足EF1和分数帕累托最优（fPO）的均衡分配，推广了Kawase等人（2026）仅对个性化双值估值或至多两种估值类型成立的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平与效率的权衡是资源分配领域的核心问题。虽然EF1和fPO等性质在一般设定下已有大量研究，但在均衡约束（要求各智能体所得商品数量尽量接近）下，同时满足这些性质的存在性问题尚未解决。已有结果仅覆盖个性化双值估值或有限类型的特殊情形，任意加性估值下的一般存在性仍是一个开放性挑战。

Method: 将Knaster-Kuratowski-Mazurkiewicz（KKM）引理应用于加权福利对偶框架，并提出了一个新的价格交错引理（price-interlacing lemma）以克服先前工作中的障碍。该方法还被推广到类别约束（即分割拟阵约束）设定，建立了类别敏感的EF1松弛下fPO分配的存在性。

Result: 在任意加性估值下，证明了满足EF1和fPO的均衡分配的存在性；并在类别约束（分割拟阵约束）下，证明了满足类别敏感EF1松弛的fPO分配的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文通过KKM引理与价格交错引理解决了均衡约束下公平且高效分配的存在性问题，显著推广了先前结果，并将技术扩展到更一般的分割拟阵约束框架。所有证明过程使用了GPT-5.6-Sol等AI辅助工具，经作者验证和简化。

Abstract: We study the existence of fair and efficient allocations of indivisible goods under the balancedness constraint, which requires that any two agents' bundles differ in size by at most one. Our main result establishes the existence of balanced allocations that satisfy envy-freeness up to one good (EF1) and fractional Pareto optimality (fPO) for arbitrary additive valuations. This generalizes a recent result of Kawase et al. (2026), which establishes existence only for personalized bivalued valuations or when there are at most two distinct valuation types. Our proof applies the Knaster-Kuratowski-Mazurkiewicz (KKM) lemma to a weighted-welfare duality framework and develops a novel price-interlacing lemma to overcome barriers encountered by prior work. We extend this technique to category constraints, also known as partition-matroid constraints. In this setting, we establish the existence of an fPO allocation satisfying a weaker, category-sensitive relaxation of EF1, under which envy can be eliminated by removing at most one good from each category. All proofs in this paper were obtained using GPT-5.6-Sol with guidance from the authors. The authors verified the proofs, expanded the exposition, and simplified the arguments with assistance from GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude Fable 5.

</details>


### [176] [Resourced Authority A Mechanism-Design Model for Participatory Governance of Deployed AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06353)
*Praphul Chandra,Sujit Gujar,Ganesh Ghalme*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于已部署AI代理的持续参与式治理的形式化机制设计模型，通过资源分配（计算预算）实现授权的自我执行，建立了"计算是有效治理杠杆"的Safe AI范式，并以博弈论框架刻画治理周期、授权门控、计算许可等核心组件，同时将"被治理代理操纵治理选民"识别为关键开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理的持续部署，需要一个持续参与式的治理机制来确保其行为安全受控。核心动机在于：治理应通过资源分配（而非代码审查或规则约束）来控制AI代理，使授权决策本身具有自我执行性（self-enforcing），从而建立计算作为有效治理杠杆的Safe AI范式。

Method: 采用机制设计与博弈论方法：(1) 将治理周期建模为扩展式博弈，验证过的利益相关者顺序到达并在供给/拒绝市场上进行贡献，使用与代理计算故意区分的治理货币；(2) 通过资金聚合器将原始贡献转化为广度加权有效支持；(3) 设计带滞回的双阈值门控（two-threshold gate with hysteresis）将净支持转为二值授权；(4) 通过受外部认证安全上限约束的耦合映射释放计量计算预算；(5) 在硬件层面实现为签名计算许可证（signed compute license），确保决策自我执行。

Result: 刻画了该机制能够治理的代理类别，并识别出核心开放问题：被治理代理对治理选民的操纵。同时提出了若干针对该操纵问题的挑战性研究方向。

Conclusion: 该工作将AI治理建模为合规层或公共地（commons）覆盖层，论证计算预算作为治理杠杆的可行性，但其安全性和鲁棒性从根本上依赖于能否解决被治理代理操纵治理选民的开放性难题。

Abstract: We give a formal mechanism design model for the continuous participatory governance of a deployed AI agent. The mechanism is built on the principle that governance should control an AI agent through resource allocation so as to make authorization self enforcing via compute budgets. The mechanism seeks to establish the Safe AI paradigm that compute is an effective governance lever. We situate our work as a compliance or commons overlay on a deployer. One governance period is an extensive form game in which verified human stakeholders arrive sequentially and contribute, on a provision or a rejection market, in a governance currency that is deliberately distinct from the agents compute. A funding aggregator turns raw contributions into breadth weighted effective supports - a two threshold gate with hysteresis converts net support into a binary authorization that, through a coupling map bounded by an exogenously certified safety ceiling, releases a metered compute budget - realized in hardware as a signed compute license so that the decision is self-enforcing. We characterize the class of agents the mechanism can govern and isolate manipulation of the governing electorate by the governed agent as the central open problem. We also introduce several challenges addressing manipulation of governing electorate by the governed agents.

</details>


### [177] [AV-AIVAT: 74x Cheaper Agent Evaluation with Certified Anytime-Valid Stopping in Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06362)
*Boning Li,Yu Chen,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-AIVAT框架，将AIVAT方差缩减技术与置信序列（CS）结合，实现不完全信息游戏中及时、有效且可审计的早期停止评估。与传统固定预算或朴素可选停止不同，该方法在保证置信水平的前提下，一旦证据充足即可停止评估。在15种LLM智能体配置、71,439手HUNL对局中，AIVAT将方差中位数降低54倍；结合AsympCS后，原始结果所需手数是AIVAT修正结果的74倍中位数；EB-CS提供精确有限样本认证，HUNL实例中停止时间比中位数为1.37倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估两个智能体孰强孰弱需要持续对局直到实力超过运气，但每局游戏都有成本。固定预算评估要么在结论已定后继续浪费资源，要么在能区分智能体之前就提前停止；而使用普通置信区间的朴素可选停止会破坏声明的置信水平。因此亟需一种既能及时停止又保持统计保证完整的评估方法。

Method: 将AIVAT（一种通过条件均值零修正降低不完全信息游戏方差的技术）与持续监控的置信序列（Confidence Sequences, CS）相结合，构造任意时有效（anytime-valid）的AV-AIVAT。其在线价值模型仅从过去对局中学习，确保每局游戏不参与自身修正。使用AsympCS进行渐近筛选，使用Empirical-Bernstein CS（EB-CS）进行精确有限样本认证，EB-CS需要独立推导的修正收益界，对Leduc hold'em建立了结构性界，并刻画了由CS下注上限与界共同决定的宽度下限。

Result: 在95%名义置信水平和±1大盲目标精度下，AIVAT修正结果比原始结果在AsympCS下中位数提前74倍停止；AIVAT在15种LLM配置、71,439手HUNL对局中将方差中位数降低54倍。EB-CS在HUNL描述性运行中显示停止时间比中位数为1.37倍。整个框架将方差削减转化为高效、可审计的早期停止。

Conclusion: AV-AIVAT成功将方差缩减转化为高效且可审计的早期停止，同时分离渐近筛选与精确认证。评估可以在证据充足的那一刻停止，并将停止时刻的一切信息交给第三方核验，实现了及时性、有效性和可审计性的统一。

Abstract: Deciding which of two agents is stronger means playing games until skill outweighs luck, and every game costs money, model inference, or expert time. Since the number of games needed is unknown, fixed-budget evaluations either keep paying after the result is settled or stop before the agents can be told apart, while naive optional stopping with an ordinary confidence interval invalidates the stated level. We make such an evaluation stop as soon as its evidence suffices, with the guarantee intact. The Action-Informed Value Assessment Tool (AIVAT) reduces variance in imperfect-information games through conditional mean-zero corrections, by a median $54\times$ across 15 LLM agent configurations spanning 71,439 paired Heads-Up No-Limit Hold'em (HUNL) hands, but does not say when to stop. We combine AIVAT with continuously monitored Confidence Sequences (CSs) into anytime-valid AIVAT (AV-AIVAT), whose online value model learns only from past games so that no game scores its own correction. At the nominal 95\% level and a target precision of $\pm1$ Big Blind, raw outcomes need a median $74\times$ as many hands as AIVAT-corrected outcomes to stop under the Asymptotic CS (AsympCS). Exact finite-sample certification uses the Empirical-Bernstein CS (EB-CS), which needs an independently justified bound on corrected payoffs. We establish such a bound structurally for Leduc hold'em and characterize a width floor set by the CS's bet cap and that bound, which governs how much of a variance gain becomes earlier stopping; the descriptive HUNL EB-CS runs show a median $1.37\times$ stopping-time ratio. AV-AIVAT turns variance reduction into efficient, auditable early stopping while separating asymptotic screening from exact certification, so an evaluation can stop the moment its evidence suffices and hand a third party everything needed to recheck the verdict at that very stopping time.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MS-MLB（多发性硬化机器学习基准），一个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的开源、可复现基准，用于MS与健康对照的机器学习分类任务。在泄漏控制的统一评估流程下，梯度提升算法以93.83的MS研究评分、0.989的AUC-ROC和0.950的灵敏度排名第一。该基准仅供研究比较，未经临床验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化（MS）的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI和实验室证据，血液RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关免疫信号，但现有研究缺乏一个开放、可复现且泄漏可控的机器学习分类基准，使得不同算法的性能比较不透明且难以重复。

Method: 利用公开GSE17048队列构建MS vs健康对照分类任务；在统一的泄漏控制流程下评估多种机器学习算法，包括嵌套交叉验证、独立分层留出集、bootstrap置信区间、ROC与PR分析、校准度量及探索性MS研究评分；框架内置外部模型提交路径。

Result: 梯度提升在留出集上排名第一，MS研究评分93.83，AUC-ROC 0.989，灵敏度0.950，特异性0.778，F1 0.927，Brier评分0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个聚焦GSE17048全血RNA表达数据MS与健康对照分类的开源基准，内置文档化的外部模型提交机制，可用于研究比较，但尚未经过临床验证。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [179] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PMOT（Potential Matching Optimal Transport），一个适用于一般p-成本最优传输（c_p(x,y)=||x-y||^p）的势流框架。PMOT用广义Benamou–Brenier形式中的标量势参数化连续归一化流（CNF）的速度场，并通过自诱导匹配损失沿模型自身端点确定的直线桥进行训练，同时允许灵活的终端分布匹配。主要理论结果证明零损失精确性：在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，任何零损失解都满足广义Benamou–Brenier最优性方程组，并能恢复对应的p-最优传输映射和动力学。在合成基准上，PMOT学到的p特定映射与对应的p-匹配OT参考一致；在高维表格数据上作为似然密度模型具有竞争力；基于MMD的颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最优传输（OT）方法通常局限于特定的成本函数（如p=2的平方欧氏距离），对一般p-成本的最优传输问题缺乏有效的可扩展求解框架。同时，连续归一化流（CNF）作为生成模型，其学习范式与最优传输原理的结合尚不充分。作者希望构建一个统一的势流框架，能够处理任意p-成本下的最优传输问题，同时在理论上保证学习解的最优性。

Method: PMOT将CNF的速度场参数化为广义Benamou–Brenier形式下的标量势，针对任意指数p构建对应的势梯度。训练过程中，模型不直接依赖真实配对，而是通过自诱导匹配损失，沿模型自身端点确定的直线桥进行训练，实现对p-成本下最优路径和终端分布的同时学习。该方法还支持灵活的终端分布匹配，可适配似然训练或基于矩/MMD的样本级匹配。

Result: 理论方面，作者证明了零损失精确性：在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，零损失解满足广义Benamou–Brenier最优性系统，恢复对应的p-最优传输映射。实验方面，在合成基准上PMOT学到的p特定映射与对应OT参考一致；在高维表格数据上作为似然密度模型具有竞争力；在MMD颜色变换实验中展示了样本级终端匹配的灵活性。

Conclusion: PMOT提供了一个统一且理论上可验证的框架，将一般p-成本最优传输与CNF势流学习相结合，兼顾了最优传输理论保证与生成模型的实际可用性。零损失精确性定理为该方法的收敛性和最优性提供了坚实的理论支撑，实验验证了其在多样任务中的有效性与灵活性。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [180] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过从冻结的预训练预测器残差中挖掘可解释的纠正性特征，驱动轻量级后置修正器，在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上超越所有专用特征工程系统，将修正器单独改进效果翻倍，并可将最弱骨干的误差降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器在结构化、重复性的场景中经常失败，通过微调修复这些失败的代价高昂。现有自动特征工程方法建模的是数据生成过程，而非模型失败过程，本文旨在纠正性地挖掘模型的失败特征，以轻量级后置修正方式修复预测残差。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网络冻结，通过两个互补的生成器挖掘残差特征：(1) 对原始输入通道进行组合搜索；(2) 使用大型语言模型(LLM)生成命名的特征组合、二元标记和短小可执行代码。所有候选特征（无论来源）都经过统一的基于验证集的门控机制接受或拒绝，再由验证集选择的修正器应用已接受特征或保持原始预测不变。该源无关管线还允许在先验特征工程系统在完全相同的条件下被评估，从而将预测改进单独归因于特征来源。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干上，CRAFTER在所有特征预算下超越每个专用特征工程系统，将仅用修正器所获得的改进约翻倍，并将最弱骨干的错误最多降低27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下保持稳健，并且在微调后的骨干之上仍然有效。

Conclusion: CRAFTER证明纠正性特征发现（即建模模型失败过程而非数据生成过程）是修复冻结预测器的一种高效且可解释的途径。其源无关架构不仅取得最优性能，还充当一个研究工具，用于将预测改进归因于特征来源本身，为后置修正场景提供了通用且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [181] [Alternating Levenberg-Marquardt Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Fourier-Enhanced Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05892)
*Yulun Wu,Matthieu Barreau,Miguel Aguiar,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FALM-PINN，一种将表示学习与系数拟合解耦的优化框架，通过傅里叶增强基函数与交替Levenberg-Marquardt算法，显著提升物理信息神经网络在高频、多尺度及强非线性PDE上的求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在求解高频率、多尺度或强非线性PDE时经常失败，根本原因在于两个因素：一是谱偏置（神经网络倾向于欠拟合高频特征），二是表示-系数耦合（表示学习与系数拟合被纠缠在单一非凸优化目标中）。

Method: FALM-PINN采用双层优化框架：上层问题学习傅里叶增强基函数，在潜空间中丰富高频成分；下层问题在该基上拟合投影系数，用Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解非线性最小二乘问题。该框架适用于一般非线性和耦合PDE系统，对于线性PDE可简化为单步凸优化问题，且两种情况下均证明了交替训练方案的全局收敛性。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的高频和非线性PDE数值实验中，FALM-PINN的相对L2误差相比最先进的基线方法降低多达两个数量级。

Conclusion: FALM-PINN通过解耦表示学习与系数拟合，有效克服了谱偏置和表示-系数耦合两大障碍，为高精度求解复杂PDE提供了一种具有全局收敛保证的优化框架。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often fail to accurately resolve partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency or multi-scale solutions, as well as strongly nonlinear problems. Two factors underlie this difficulty: spectral bias, the tendency of neural networks to underfit high-frequency features; and representation-coefficient coupling, the entanglement of representation learning and coefficient fitting within a single nonconvex optimization objective. In this work, we propose the Fourier-enhanced alternating Levenberg--Marquardt PINN (FALM-PINN), an optimization framework that decouples representation learning from coefficient fitting. The upper-level problem learns a Fourier-enhanced basis that enriches the latent space with high-frequency components, while the lower-level problem resolves the coupling by fitting the projection coefficients on this basis, solving a nonlinear least-squares problem with the Levenberg--Marquardt algorithm. The framework applies to general nonlinear and coupled PDE systems, and reduces to a single-step convex optimization problem for linear PDEs. We prove global convergence of the alternating training scheme in both cases. Numerical examples on multiple challenging high-frequency and nonlinear PDEs show that FALM-PINN achieves relative $L^2$ errors up to two orders of magnitude lower than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [182] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程基于大语言模型（LLM）流程的概率性语言，使开发者能够量化并传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并无需额外代码即可实验不同的推理扩展技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建依赖LLM的可靠应用仍具挑战，LLM输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量，多次调用LLM和其他工具会累积不确定性，使开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 引入一种概率性编程语言来构建LLM流程，允许开发者量化并传播不确定性，同时支持在不需要修改流程逻辑代码的情况下尝试不同的推理扩展（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该能力的有效性，并通过构建一个针对Rocq定理证明器的定理证明agent进行了案例研究。

Conclusion: 该概率性语言为LLM-based应用提供了一种系统化的不确定性管理和推理扩展方法，有助于提升应用的可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [183] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kastor是一种将确定性物理基础模型转化为高效生成式代理模型的方法，通过两阶段推理（大步长自回归+非因果时间超分辨率）、均值预测正则化（MPR）和空间梯度匹配三种组件，在The Well基准上较Walrus方法平均降低42.9%的预测误差，并在10个数据集中8个VRMSE指标上优于Walrus。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习虽可加速物理模拟以替代昂贵的传统PDE求解器，但标准自回归ML模拟器在长时程预测上存在误差累积问题，且难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性。

Method: Kastor包含三个核心组件：(1) 两阶段推理方案——大步长因果自回归模型配合非因果时间超分辨率网络，在降低计算成本的同时减少误差累积；(2) 均值预测正则化（MPR）——新的训练目标，在零噪声条件下约束生成模型预测确定性分布均值，提升FGN和扩散模拟器的性能与稳定性；(3) 空间梯度匹配——通过匹配空间梯度提升模拟的物理保真度（以功率谱密度衡量）。

Result: 在The Well基准的多种模拟数据集上，Kastor在预测精度、谱一致性和计算效率方面均优于竞争对手。相比基于Walrus微调方法，预测误差平均降低42.9%，且10个数据集中有8个的方差归一化RMSE（VRMSE）优于Walrus。

Conclusion: Kastor通过两阶段推理、MPR正则化和梯度匹配三个组件的协同作用，成功构建了高效且准确的生成式物理模拟代理模型，在精度、谱一致性和效率上全面超越现有方法。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [184] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CGTime模型，通过将感知与描述解耦（确定性代码计算统计量，LLM负责语言表达）来克服多模态时间序列-语言对齐中的自我监督陷阱，4B参数的模型在多变量时间序列理解任务上优于GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5.4-nano等更大规模的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练多模态时间序列-语言模型的方法存在两个关键问题：一是自我监督陷阱——让LLM读取序列并写描述，标签质量受限于模型本身的感知能力，数据无法教会模型超出标签者已知的内容；二是大多数数据集只使用单变量，而真正重要的模式（跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）只出现在多变量场景中，恰好暴露了标注LLM的极限。这两个问题导致三难困境：现有方法在可靠性、真实性和可扩展性中只能兼得其一。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：使用确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算一组统计量，再由LLM对这些预计算的事实进行语言化描述。感知（LLM不擅长）由计算处理，表达由LLM负责。由此构建了4B参数的CGTime（computation-grounded time-series-language model）。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上优于更大的通用模型：在保留基准数据集上取得最佳多变量事实分数（0.283，对比GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），该优势通过Holm校正配对显著性检验。生成的描述中可验证数值事实更准确，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过将统计计算从语言表达中解耦，可以同时实现可靠性、真实性和可扩展性，打破了三难困境。计算接地的方法让更小的专业模型在多变量时间序列理解上超越更大的通用模型。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [185] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的空间推理范式DiSR，将3D感知与推理明确解耦：利用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据，再通过LoRA微调的LLM仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理。无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略，DiSR在主流空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时具备更高的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过大规模训练隐式地将3D感知与推理联合学习，但感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，而LLM擅长组合与符号推理。作者观察到二者优势互补，提出将两者解耦以利用各自所长，而非在端到端模型中混合学习。

Method: DiSR框架包含两个组件：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据；(2) 通过LoRA对LLM进行微调，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据进行空间推理。该方法不依赖大规模3D VQA预训练或复杂工具调用策略。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上，DiSR取得了与端到端方法竞争的性能，同时展现出更强的可解释性、模块化能力和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能中一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，表明无需端到端建模即可实现高质量的空间推理能力。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [186] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了因子化生成模型中一个常见误区：将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验并不能保证其与类别信息独立。作者推导出精确分解，证明边缘匹配只是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一，并通过大量实验验证了这一不足——即使全局MMD接近零，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（10%为随机水平）。四种缓解策略可将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖仍基本不变；后验条件先验和风格库方法分别在不同数据集上取得了不同程度的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型广泛采用将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验的做法，并将其解释为风格表示与类别信息独立的证据。作者指出这一解释在数学上不成立——边缘分布匹配对类条件分布没有任何约束，风格潜变量可能在整体上呈现完美高斯分布的同时仍然高度携带类别信息。本文旨在严格证明这一误区的理论根源并提供系统的实证验证，从而纠正该领域的错误共识。

Method: 作者首先推导出一个精确的数学分解，证明边缘分布匹配仅是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一，消除该不匹配是必要但不充分的条件。随后，作者在一个案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在基线模型上进行实证测试，使用全局MMD评估边缘分布匹配程度，同时使用线性探针测量类别信息的泄漏。实验涵盖六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督信号）和两个数据集。最后，作者测试了四种缓解策略（降低探针准确率至21%-46%）以及两种后处理方案（后验条件先验和实证风格库）。

Result: 实验结果表明：所有模型在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（10%为随机水平）；案例研究模型达到99.15%的聚类准确率，但外部评估的类条件生成仅成功16%。六种扰动下泄漏现象持续存在。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%但类内依赖基本不变。后验条件先验在MNIST上实现外部类生成0.97无需重新训练，但在CIFAR-10上仅达0.41；实证风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度无法证明其与类别标签的独立性，仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型宣称的性质。这一发现对现有因子化生成模型的评估标准提出了根本性质疑，要求该领域必须采用能够捕捉类条件分布的评估方法或显式的条件约束来实现真正的因子化。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [187] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成流程生成带优先级的多规则多模态指令数据（规则本理解任务），并配套PRISM-Eval评估基准（含Loose和Strict指标，无需推理时评判模型）。仅用1万条合成样本，将Qwen3-VL-4B在Strict准确率上从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准性能，且增益可迁移至五个开源MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态指令通常捆绑多个重要性不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据大多将指令遵循简化为回答单个自包含问题。作者通过"规则本理解"（rubric comprehension）研究这一差距：模型不应被当作对照规则来评判的生成器，而应作为遵循规则的执行器。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：(1) 生成persona-task配对；(2) 前缀引导的规则集生成；(3) 质量过滤的规则本；(4) 结构化验证轨迹。同时引入PRISM-Eval评估集，使用Loose和Strict两种指标，通过固定标签的确定性匹配进行评分，无需推理时评判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能；增益可迁移至另外四个开源MLLM，覆盖密集和MoE两种架构。

Conclusion: 结构化规则本监督是多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的一种可扩展路径。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT提出了一种参数高效的框架，将多任务监督微调(SFT)中的过拟合感知调度状态（如roll-out、exclusion和rollback）从全模型检查点重新表示为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器，在五个骨干网络上取得优于原始msft方法的平均准确率（61.36% vs 59.85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务SFT通常将所有异构数据混合作为单一优化问题处理，但不同任务在不同时间达到最佳泛化。msft通过任务级roll-out、exclusion和rollback解决了这一不匹配问题，但其原始实现将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换时存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器；在任务级峰值处回滚适配器检查点，并继续处理剩余的活跃任务混合。在层级别，每个适配器采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层，将其应用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示；使得更新对输入保持线性、秩有界，并能精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干网络比较协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率为61.36%，对比msft参考行（59.85%），且在全部五个骨干网络上均获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过将msft的调度状态压缩为可合并的适配器状态，在保持过拟合感知多任务微调有效性的同时，显著降低了存储、恢复和部署的代价，并在多个骨干网络上展示了性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT方法，在量化正交微调（QOFT）基础上附加零起点门控低秩非线性残差分支，在保持量化兼容稳定性的同时引入输入依赖的非线性修正能力，在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B模型上相比QOFT提升1.30-2.70%的Macro-6指标，相比QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，并节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化正交微调（QOFT）虽然能实现低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换内，缺乏输入依赖的非线性修正能力，这限制了其表达能力和适配效果。

Method: AuroOFT保留QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零起点门控低秩非线性残差。该方法将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间，使用自适应非线性基函数与有界或token依赖的门控机制。零初始化的上投影确保AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT功能等价，且正交性仅作为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的整体属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B设置上相比匹配的QOFT提升Macro-6指标1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模上相比QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过在QOFT基础上引入零起点门控低秩非线性残差，成功将非线性修正能力注入量化微调框架，同时保持初始化的功能等价性和分支级稳定性，实现了更优的参数效率和任务性能。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [190] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出科学解释可容许性机器（SEAM），一个与生成器无关的框架，将局部解释的一致性验证扩展为全局可计算问题。通过有限解释层（SEAM-Ω）的实例化，该方法用状态、闭包、观测通道和可选契约元数据表示每个区域，在重叠处比较相邻解释，并将不一致转化为通道解析的障碍（obstruction），实现全局解释一致性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习通常在子域、基准分割或单次预测层面验证模型，但这种局部检查无法确定解释能否组装成全局可容许的整体解释。现有的验证方法缺乏从局部判断到全局一致性的可计算框架，导致需要一种方法使局部到全局的一致性检查变得可计算。

Method: SEAM框架采用两个关键设计：1）有限解释层SEAM-Ω，将每个区域表示为包含状态、闭包、观测通道及可选契约元数据的结构化解释；2）在区域重叠处比较相邻解释，将不一致转化为通道解析的障碍，通过限制每个修复的修订范围来测试竞争性声明账户，精确可行性判定拒绝或保留账户，精确修复不可用时使用残差感知正则化记录。框架还区分不一致与不可识别性，并监控生成器在分布偏移下的行为。

Result: 建立了最小代价干预和守恒契约可检测性定理，以及可识别性和闭包可恢复性的伴随结果。在19个实验中（包括合成偏微分方程系统和分布外傅里叶神经算子监测），SEAM在局部预测准确的情况下仍能检测出不相容的解释，并将失败归因于特定通道和重叠区域。

Conclusion: SEAM为现有求解器和学习模型增加了全局解释一致性审计，检验其局部解释能否形成一致的科学生态解释，填补了局部验证与全局可容许性之间的空白。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [191] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向散户投资者的、已完整构建并通过集成测试的税务感知型智能投顾应用，包含FastAPI后端与网页前端，将自然语言投资目标路由至六个投资指令之一，并由一个三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、带意图路由器的MoE配置策略、基于个人券商行为进行个性化LoRA适配器）生成实时代理人集成的投资组合推荐。系统功能完整、已对实时券商API完成端到端集成测试，但尚未对真实用户开放，属于预部署阶段，并提供14天滚动回测及若干工程经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户所享有的个性化、税务感知的投资组合管理服务：现有机器人投顾依赖静态规则化配置，而机构级系统的最低账户门槛和技术栈令个人投资者难以企及。作者旨在缩小这一差距，构建一个可供个人投资者使用的、具备机构级智能化水平的应用。

Method: 系统采用FastAPI后端与网页仪表盘架构，用户以自然语言描述投资目标，系统将该目标路由到六个投资指令之一。核心为三阶段强化学习系统：(1) 自监督跨资产编码器；(2) 带学习意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）配置策略；(3) 轻量级LoRA适配器，从个人经纪行为中个性化推荐而无需重训共享模型。系统集成多用户认证、预览-确认信任流程、每日邮件摘要及可审计的操作完整性链，并对Alpaca实时API完成端到端集成测试。

Result: 系统功能完备并通过端到端集成测试（含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、邮件摘要、审计链），但尚未向真实用户开放。作者如实报告其为预部署的涌现应用，并提供14天滚动回测（含自助法置信区间）作为预部署验证而非生产性能指标。同时报告了三项可推广的工程经验：静默失效的集成路径、挂起的第三方API调用，以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于构建了一个面向散户的、税务感知且个性化的智能投顾应用，通过三阶段强化学习架构（自监督编码器+MoE路由策略+LoRA个性化适配）实现机构级功能在个人场景的落地。作者诚实地标注了其预部署状态，提供了从预部署到全面部署的可行路径，并提炼了适用于其他基于外部实时数据源的RL系统的工程经验。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [192] [Accelerating nanodrug development in continuous flow systems using informed prediction models based on low-cost surrogate nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05761)
*Kai Dahms,Eilien Heinrich,Jochen Schmid,Michael Bortz,Iryna Savych,Regina Bleul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出并验证了一种基于形状约束的预测建模方法，用于微流控制备脂质体和脂质纳米颗粒时，在多种工艺条件下准确预测纳米粒子的尺寸和多分散指数（PDI），从而减少传统经验性筛选实验的依赖，提高纳米药物治疗开发效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米治疗药物的开发依赖大量经验性优化实验，因为纳米粒子性质（如尺寸和PDI）对工艺参数变化高度敏感，且缺乏预测性数学模型，导致开发成本高、周期长。

Method: 采用基于形状约束的预测建模方法，结合实验数据与专家知识，在受控微流控条件下系统制备脂质体和脂质纳米颗粒，改变脂质浓度、流速和水相/有机相混合比等工艺参数，以最少实验数据构建并验证模型。

Result: 形状约束模型能够准确预测纳米粒子的尺寸和分散性，减少了对大量实验筛选的依赖，并在药物应用中仅用极少经验数据即可完成验证。

Conclusion: 形状约束建模框架支持纳米药物制造中合理且高效的过程开发，为理性设计纳米治疗系统提供了可行的预测性工具。

Abstract: The development of nanotherapeutics often involves extensive empirical optimization due to the sensitivity of nanoparticle properties, such as size and polydispersity index (PDI), to minor changes in process parameters. Factors like formulation concentration, flow rates, and mixing ratios can significantly influence clinical efficacy and therapeutic outcomes. The absence of predictive mathematical frameworks has made iterative experimental screening necessary, increasing both costs and development time.
  This study introduces and validates a predictive modeling approach based on shape constraints, aiming to enhance the estimation of nanoparticle characteristics across various process conditions. Using controlled microfluidic methods, liposomes and lipid nanoparticles were systematically prepared under varying lipid concentrations, flow rates, and aqueous-to-organic mixing ratios.
  The shape-constrained model, informed by both experimental data and expert knowledge, was subsequently validated for a pharmaceutical application using minimal empirical data. Results reveal that shape-constrained modeling facilitates accurate prediction of nanoparticle size and dispersity, reducing the need for extensive experimental workflows. This framework supports rational and efficient process development for manufacturing nanomedicine systems.

</details>


### [193] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）用于预测火灾后泥石流事件，发现TabPFN在未增强数据上表现最佳（威胁分数0.637），SHAP分析显示短期降雨强度和风暴累积量是最关键的特征，并通过TabPFN合成数据增强有效提升了大多数模型的性能（深度学习模型平均提升+0.041）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火灾后泥石流预测对降低社区、基础设施和资源风险至关重要，但存在三大挑战：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中重叠导致模型区分困难、模型可解释性需求高、训练数据有限。现有研究缺乏对这些挑战的系统性评估。

Method: 使用美国西部流域尺度的火灾后泥石流观测数据，对比15种机器学习模型（包含新提出的TabPFN模型），采用重复分层交叉验证评估性能，结合SHAP进行特征重要性归因分析，并利用TabPFN生成合成样本来进行数据增强以缓解样本稀缺问题。

Result: TabPFN在未增强数据上取得最高威胁分数0.637，优于最佳树模型；SHAP揭示短期降雨强度和风暴累积量为预测首要特征，燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较小；TabPFN合成数据增强提升了除CNN外所有模型的性能，其中深度学习模型平均威胁分数提升最大为+0.041。

Conclusion: 本研究通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，提供了一个全面改进火灾后泥石流预测的框架，为实际灾害预警和风险管理提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [194] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSPDIM，一种基于SPD流形的在线源无关无监督域适应框架，通过信息最大化优化流形约束的偏置参数来纠正类别不平衡导致的几何错位，解决EEG-BCI跨被试/跨会话中的标签偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的脑机接口需要无监督域适应来跨被试和跨会话泛化，虽然黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）能有效处理协变量偏移，但它们隐式假设类别先验平衡。在实际在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术在几何上错误对齐目标数据分布，因此需要针对标签偏移的在线适应方法。

Method: OSPDIM是源自由的在线UDA框架，核心创新包括：(1) 在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数；(2) 通过信息最大化优化该参数以纠正不平衡数据流引起的几何歪斜；(3) 在线估计和纠正几何偏置，不依赖全局批量统计。

Result: 在二维SPD矩阵上的仿真实验可视化验证了OSPDIM能够纠正标准居中方法无法处理的错位问题。在多个运动想象数据集上的广泛实验表明，OSPDIM在严重类别不平衡的在线适应场景中显著优于标准黎曼基线方法。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际且即插即用的BCI系统提供了鲁棒解决方案，有效解决了在线场景下由标签偏移引起的黎曼几何错位问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [195] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案，用可恢复的逐出替代二元保留-删除策略，解决自回归大语言模型推理中KV缓存的内存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于注意力分数的token逐出策略做出不可逆的删除决定，但在解码过程中token和窗口的重要性会随生成查询的变化而漂移，导致被逐出的状态可能在后续获得大量注意力关注，造成信息永久丢失。

Method: 引入两个诊断指标（Future Missed Mass和Global LIR）来量化被丢弃状态获得的未来注意力及历史非活跃区域的重新激活。提出QEvict三层KV缓存管理：高置信度窗口保持全精度，中间窗口存储在量化可恢复层，仅删除最低置信度窗口。解码时通过累积注意力分数更新窗口重要性，量化窗口重新变重要时被反量化和提升到全精度。

Result: 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上，QEvict持续优于代表性的逐出和量化基线，减少了遗漏的注意力并改善了信息保留。

Conclusion: 固定内存预算下，QEvict的三层设计能在保留更广泛历史上下文的同时，为最重要的区域保持精确的全精度表示，克服了传统不可逆逐出策略的局限性。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [196] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse，一种模拟器层面的联邦学习机制，允许选定的客户端在随机头梯度差异代理保持在轮次依赖阈值以下时贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新。通过硬缓存年龄限制和最少新鲜客户端配额约束重用，新鲜更新则使用自适应逐张量Top-K数值域表示。在六类图像数据集上，以90轮预算实现83.36-85.42%的上行更新数据域节省，但纳入对称密集模型下行链路敏感性后节省降至41.68-42.71%。作者明确声明该机制不建立无偏泛化或端到端带宽/运行时/能量节省等保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习反复进行局部优化和模型更新传输，通信开销是主要瓶颈。现有方法（如Top-K FedAvg）虽能减少上行数据，但未充分利用客户端的历史（缓存）更新。作者旨在探索在模拟器层面利用年龄衰减的缓存更新结合Top-K稀疏化来进一步降低上行通信成本，同时明确该研究是机制特性刻画而非端到端性能论证。

Method: DG-FedReuse采用以下核心设计：（1）随机头梯度差异代理（stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy）作为是否允许客户端重用缓存更新的判定条件，并与轮次依赖阈值比较；（2）硬缓存年龄限制（hard cache-age limit）和最少新鲜客户端配额（minimum fresh-client quota）双重约束重用行为；（3）新鲜客户端更新采用自适应逐张量Top-K数值域表示（adaptive per-tensor Top-K）进行稀疏化传输；（4）实验配置：六类图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性α=0.5、三个随机种子，以90轮为公共预算。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的建模更新数据域上行节省，优于匹配Top-K FedAvg的76.88%；种子对齐的准确率差异在-5.29到-0.14个百分点之间。最佳观测测试准确率（在测试受控检查点下）相对匹配FedAvg为-2.38到+0.45个百分点。纳入对称密集模型下行链路后，头部节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对Top-K FedAvg的增量增益仅为3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse在模拟器层面展示了基于年龄衰减缓存重用加Top-K稀疏化的上行通信节省潜力，其效果严重依赖通信会计边界（是否计入下行链路）。研究仅刻画了所提出重用规则在模拟器中的行为特征，不声称建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽/运行时/能量节省、更快收敛或对现有陈旧更新和惰性聚合方法的优越性。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [197] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构化世界模型（QSWMs），一种受量子理论启发的世界建模框架，采用复数表示和密度矩阵类潜变量，并验证了其在元胞自动机预测任务上的潜力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐状态或Transformer激活来表征潜状态，作者希望探索量子理论启发的数学结构（如复数表示和密度矩阵类潜变量）是否能为世界建模提供有用的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出QSWM框架，包含结构化潜状态、潜转移算子和测量启发的解码映射；证明三个基础性质（经典包含性、预测充分性、结构紧凑性）；实例化复数型与密度矩阵型两种QSWM变体，在初等元胞自动机上与强经典基线比较。

Result: 复数型QSWM在局部预测中表现出有前景的潜力，但长时域展开和密度矩阵变体存在局限性。

Conclusion: 量子启发的结构化表示对世界建模具有部分价值，但当前方法在长时域预测和密度矩阵变体上仍需改进。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [198] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一条可证明的流水线，通过凸学习方法（Observation Spectral Filtering）从非线性动力系统的观测中学习隐式谱预测器，再通过谱到LDS蒸馏将其显式化为紧凑的线性动力系统，无需直接求解非凸的系统辨识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统通常难以直接学习，传统方法需要求解非凸的系统辨识问题，缺乏理论保证。作者希望找到一种从非线性系统观测中提取线性状态空间表示的端到端可证明方法，且保证与观测者复杂度而非潜在维度相关。

Method: 采用两步流水线：(1) 使用Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF)这一凸方法学习与最优线性观测者竞争的隐式谱预测器；(2) 通过谱到LDS蒸馏（spectral-to-LDS distillation）将隐式预测器转化为显式的循环线性动力系统。主定理将蒸馏LDS的预测误差分解为指数小的蒸馏项和受Luenberger复杂度约束的OSF学习项。

Result: 理论结果表明预测误差具有维度无关的保证，仅依赖观测者复杂度而非非线性系统的潜在表示维度。实验在线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务上验证，训练后蒸馏的流水线生成的紧凑LDS预测器能匹配或超越直接训练的基线模型。

Conclusion: 这是首个通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏，从非线性动力学中提取最优事后（best-in-hindsight）LDS表示的端到端可证明方法，同时也为非线性系统的可验证线性表示提供了新途径。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [199] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需组标注即可识别受虚假相关性影响的样本的方法，利用收敛后模型的扰动敏感性信号来区分符合虚假相关性的样本（共享决策规则、更鲁棒）与不符合的样本（个体化拟合、更脆弱）。该方法只需每个训练样本两次前向传播，无需组标注或早停，在Waterbirds上将最差组准确率从57.3%提升到80.8%（接近使用真实组标签的85.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化训练的模型在包含虚假相关性的数据上取得高平均准确率，但在相关性不成立的子群体上表现差。现有无需组标注的识别方法依赖训练早期信号，需要选择介入的epoch，这通常需要带组标签的验证数据来调参，限制了实用性。

Method: 作者观察到在模型收敛后（此时损失不再区分两类群体），仍存在可用信号：与虚假相关性一致的样本被一个共享规则分类，而其余样本通过针对各输入的具体配置被拟合，后者更脆弱。因此对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，统计预测翻转情况——脆弱样本（不符合虚假相关性）的预测翻转频率远高于前者。整个过程每个训练样本只需两次前向传播。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，使用检测到的样本进行重平衡训练，最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用真实组标签的上限为85.8%，表明该方法达到接近有监督组标注的性能。

Conclusion: 收敛后的模型输入扰动可以提供可靠的虚假相关性样本识别信号，无需组标注、无需早停epoch选择，是一种简单实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [200] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFNs），将生成流网络框架（AFlowNets）从不完全信息博弈扩展到不完全信息博弈，证明原有约束在不完全信息设置下无法获得有效策略密度和训练目标，并通过新泛化形式IFlowNets缓解该问题。初步实验表明其在标准博弈环境中性能与速度优于或持平OSMCCFR和标准RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量工作将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以权衡计算速度与性能，但在不完全信息博弈中，对生成式采样框架的探索仍然较少。作者希望将生成流网络（GFlowNets）这一生成采样框架扩展到不完全信息博弈场景，弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 1) 将对抗流网络（AFlowNets）扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络（IFNs）。2) 证明完整信息博弈中既有的生成流网络约束在不完全信息设置下无法得到有效密度（对应玩家策略）和有效训练目标。3) 提出IFlowNets作为AFlowNets的严格泛化，以解决上述不可行性问题。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境中，IFlowNets的性能和速度与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）及标准RL方法相比，表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功将生成流网络框架推广到不完全信息博弈，既保证了理论上的有效性，又在实证中展现出与传统博弈求解方法（如OSMCCFR）和RL方法可比或更优的性能，为该方向提供了可行的新框架。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [201] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"自由（freedom）"才是生成建模的第三个预训练轴，而非探索（exploration）。XM（Explorative Modeling）通过产生K个输出并只更新最接近的一个，实际上是在优化行为的自由性而非生成表现力。自由是行为的属性而非形式的属性，最弱约束的模型最可能泛化。理论证明XM损失取决于候选者错过可接受区域的概率，探索使该概率降为K次幂；实证表明XM确实优化自由，自由选择器在30个案例中以29胜的优势胜过验证选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式训练中，单候选比较会使偏向公共答案的模型获得更好表现，从而抑制模型的表达多样性。XM通过每次比较产生K个输出并更新最接近者来声称"探索"是第三个预训练轴，但本文认为这一轴的本质是"自由"（约束的强弱），而非探索本身。

Method: 理论推导部分：证明平均XM损失取决于候选者错过可接受区域的概率，探索将该概率提升至K次幂；对K>1证明匹配概率随自由增加。实证部分：进行Forward XM实验，验证更大K是否增加或饱和测量自由；训练XM候选池，将验证选择与仅读取无标签父上下文的自由选择器进行比较。

Result: 更大K增加或饱和了测量自由，且在上下文相关目标下每个测试值都增加自由；自由选择器在30个案例中赢得29个。理论证明自由选择在归纳实验中比MDL优110-500%。

Conclusion: 生成表现力只是自由在模式数上的代理指标，丢弃了自由泛化意义所依赖的外延结构。XM是手段，自由是目的——在分布偏移下选择自由可以改进XM。自由是最弱约束模型的属性，最弱模型最可能泛化，这为生成建模提供了一个比表现力或MDL更本质的优化目标。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [202] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoHarness-RL框架，将外部harness状态（信念、进度、经验）作为可学习的策略接口，通过监督微调教授harness动作空间，再结合成本感知GRPO训练协调策略，实现长期任务中的选择性外部状态访问。在ALFWorld上以Qwen3-8B达到96.9%成功率，并揭示了harness退火和harness演化两种关键动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体依赖外部执行支持（状态维护、进度追踪、工具调用、结果验证、经验复用），但有效使用harness面临两个耦合挑战：从噪声交互轨迹中形成状态，以及对外部状态访问的运行时控制。现有方法依赖提示词、启发式或领域特定约定手工设计外部工作空间和使用策略，缺乏可学习的策略来协调外部状态。

Method: 提出harness策略学习问题，将外部harness状态建模为信念（Belief）、进度（Progress）、经验（Experience）三类策略可见状态。采用两阶段训练：先用监督harness微调教授基础智能体harness动作空间及外部状态构建方法，再用成本感知GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）探索协调策略，使智能体在长时程交互中选择性读取、更新和整合状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld基准上以Qwen3-8B为基座实现96.9%的成功率。发现两种关键动态：(1) harness退火——训练将重复的harness使用模式内化到模型策略中，使智能体从频繁harness调用转向选择性外部状态访问；(2) harness演化——进度更新和经验整合将harness精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基底。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体受益于可训练的harness构建与协调策略，而非仅仅依赖更强的工具或更大的记忆容量。外部harness工作空间及其使用策略可以通过离线学习获得，为长时程智能体设计提供新的思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [203] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HProbZ，一种基于zonotope的神经概率预测头，将不确定性分解为离散选择、有界系统漂移和随机噪声三类，通过共享有界生成器实现代数耦合，支持闭式似然、逐模式风险和分布自由的多模态共形预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测头要么输出高斯混合，要么输出单一共形区域，无法分离真实预测任务中常见的三类不确定性来源：离散模态选择、模态内有界系统漂移、不可约随机噪声。这种混叠限制了对不确定性结构的理解和利用。

Method: 引入Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ)输出头，将三类不确定性分别表示为zonotope的二元生成器、有界生成器和随机生成器，通过卷积获得闭式似然。关键设计是跨预测步骤共享有界生成器，使未来预测代数量地耦合，观测一步即可单次前向传播精化所有剩余步骤的预测分布。

Result: 理论上证明了三类生成器可从似然中识别（模置换），且HProbZ密度与任何有限高斯混合在表示上不同。同时提供了解析的逐模式风险和分布自由的多模态共形集。在代表性预测基准上，相对同编码器混合基线验证了设计有效性，并具备混合或凸共形预测器无法同时提供的结构性质。

Conclusion: HProbZ有效融合了三类不确定性的显式建模能力与共享代数耦合的结构优势，兼具闭式推断、可识别性和分布自由共形保证，为多模态概率预测提供了混合预测器与共形预测器互补的新路径。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [204] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出矩阵Zonotopic注意力（MZAttn），通过上下文自适应的值族替代标准多头注意力中固定的值投影，以克服标准注意力在处理置换不变集合目标时由于"上下文刚性"（context-rigidity）导致的深度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头注意力中，输入相关的软路由与输入无关的线性值投影不对称，导致聚合值到输出的算子映射对所有输入集相同（上下文刚性）。这种不对称性限制了模型表示置换不变集合目标的能力，需要与目标复杂度成比例的深度。
