However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [237] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对二值神经网络的后训练早停机制：利用训练集上运行累加和的行为特征，在累加过程中尽早预测最终符号，从而跳过后续的累加操作，无需重新训练任何模型参数。在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深层卷积可跳过86.6%的累加项（精度下降0.37点），同时作用于三个最深层卷积时可减少25%的全网络算术操作（精度下降1.36点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络在受限部署场景中有吸引力，但二值激活下的点积虽变为符号控制的加减法，运算次数并未减少——每个神经元仍需累加所有输入，即使只保留符号。累加过程中，部分和经常偏离零很远，最终符号在最后一项到达之前就已高度可预测，此后的贡献只改变和的数值而不改变输出激活，造成计算浪费。

Method: 将上述观察转化为后训练早停机制。在训练数据集上刻画运行累加和的行为特征，利用这些信息尽早预测最终符号。不重新训练任何模型参数，并统计理想权重排序下的运算次数。

Result: 在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深层卷积移除86.6%的累加项，精度仅下降0.37点；当同时应用于三个最深层卷积时，减少25%的全网络算术操作，精度下降1.36点。

Conclusion: 通过利用二值神经网络累加过程中符号可预测性的先验统计特征，可以在保持精度几乎不变的前提下大幅减少计算开销，为二值网络的部署提供了一种轻量且无需重训练的后训练优化手段。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [238] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGA（Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment），一种基于依赖解析器监督的偏好优化框架，用解析器判断替代人工偏好标注，通过delta-DPO、复合奖励、奖励差距过滤和奖励黑客监控，在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威博克马尔语三种低资源语言上显著提升生成文本的语法质量，证明解析器派生监督可作为低资源语言中人工偏好标注的实用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化方法虽然对改进大语言模型有效，但严重依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注。对于形态丰富且资源稀缺的语言，人工标注数据尤其匮乏，这使得现有偏好优化方法难以直接扩展到这些语言。因此需要一种不依赖人工标注的替代方案。

Method: SAGA框架包括四个关键组件：(1) 将依赖解析器的判断转换为偏好对用于delta-DPO训练；(2) 构建结合解析器质量与词汇多样性的复合奖励函数；(3) 使用奖励差距准则过滤低信息量的偏好对；(4) 监控奖励黑客行为以维持可靠的监督信号。

Result: 在GPT-SW3-1.3B上对丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威博克马尔语进行实验，SAGA无需人工偏好标签即可一致提升语法质量。丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升至93.8%；冰岛语在独立的Stanza评估上平均提升+4.5个百分点（三次运行均值+3.3个百分点），且母语者在80%的成对比较中更偏好SAGA输出；挪威博克马尔语提升+28个百分点。

Conclusion: SAGA证明了在高质量依赖解析器可用的低资源语言中，解析器派生监督是人工偏好标注的实用替代方案，能有效实现语法对齐。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [239] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三轴框架（When/How/Where）来刻画持续学习从参数中心化学习向系统级适应的转变，涵盖离策略/在策略/超越梯度的优化机制、训练前/训练后/推理时的时序阶段，以及内部参数与外部结构约束的更新位置，系统综述了代表性方法并讨论了范式转变带来的挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习主要关注参数中心的机制（如训练策略、架构设计、权重适应），但新兴范式如on-policy学习、test-time training以及外部构建组件（记忆、技能库、交互协议）正在将持续学习的边界从静态参数空间扩展到系统级适应，需要对这一转变进行系统性刻画。

Method: 提出三维分析框架：How维度涵盖off-policy、on-policy和超越梯度的优化机制；When维度涵盖pre-training、post-training和inference-time阶段；Where维度区分内部参数更新与外部结构约束的更新。基于该框架对代表性方法进行系统综述。

Result: 通过三轴框架系统梳理了持续学习领域的代表性方法，追踪了从参数中心化学习向系统级适应的演变轨迹，并识别出该范式转变带来的关键挑战和未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 持续学习正在经历从参数中心化到系统级适应的范式转变，通过When/How/Where三维框架可以更好地理解这一演变，并为后续研究提供分析视角。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [240] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3是一个轻量级概率全球天气预报模型（约1.34亿参数），通过显式多时间步推理，用单套权重在推理时灵活配置模型时间步长，从而在短时间步（细粒度但误差累积多）和长时间步（误差累积少但分辨率低）之间取得权衡，实现近SOTA的中期概率预报技能、稳定的延伸期滚动预测以及高效训练推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型使用预先固定的自回归时间步长，面临根本性权衡：短时间步（1-6小时）能精细解析日循环内的大气动力学，但在给定预报时效内会加剧误差累积；长时间步（如24小时）减少误差累积，但限制了短临预报中亚日尺度的可用性。作者旨在解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 提出GEM-3，一个基于邻域注意力变换器的轻量级概率全球天气预报模型（约1.34亿参数，等距圆柱网格）。其核心创新是显式多时间步推理：使用单套训练权重，推理时可配置模型时间步长以权衡可预测性与实用性。此外，采用混合时间步训练，相比时间步专家模型一致性地改善了滚动稳定性。模型包含了超越前代GEM-2的多项架构改进。

Result: GEM-3在中期概率预报技能上达到接近最先进（SOTA）的水平；支持稳定的延伸期滚动预测；训练和推理高效；能够输出与决策相关的诊断信息。

Conclusion: GEM-3通过单套权重的多时间步推理和混合时间步训练，成功解决了固定时间步的权衡问题，构建了一个集近SOTA中期概率技能、稳定延伸期滚动、高效训练推理与决策相关诊断于一体的实用天气预报系统。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [241] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了后训练适应（post-training adaptation）领域的文献，提出一个六维分类体系（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），用于统一描述和比较微调、检索增强、模型编辑、对齐等技术，并揭示技术间的继承、替代、混合与分层堆叠关系，最终指出评估、可复现性、推理时适应、多模态适应等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练适应技术在现代机器学习实践中日益重要，但该领域文献在不同技术族、模型类别和部署场景之间高度碎片化，缺乏统一的比较框架和描述语言，导致难以对比不同方法或准确描述模型被如何修改。

Method: 采用综述方法，综合后训练适应文献，提出一个六维分类体系，具体包含机制（mechanism）、目标（goal）、数据需求（data requirement）、持久性（persistence）、结构范围（structural scope）和模型类型（model type）六个维度；同时厘清微调、检索增强和提示（prompting）等常被混淆的概念，并绘制技术间的关系图谱（包括继承、替代、混合和分层部署堆叠）。

Result: 构建了一个统一的六维分类体系，区分了常被混淆的术语；展示了适应策略从传统机器学习、深度学习、基础模型、大语言模型到多模态大语言模型的演进路径；绘制了技术间的关系（继承、替代、混合、分层部署栈），形成可支持技术文档编写、模型变更追踪和治理分析的词汇表。

Conclusion: 该综述为后训练适应提供了统一的分类词汇与关系映射，可支撑模型治理与分析；同时识别出开放挑战，包括评估、可复现性、持久性推理时适应、反学习（unlearning）、多模态适应以及面向治理的后训练工作流等方面。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [242] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaboLLM，一个通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索适配的代谢组学专用大语言模型，以及MetaboLLM-GIN，将生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图用于患者级预测。在四个基座模型家族上，MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上均优于基线模型；MetaboLLM-GIN在应激性高血糖预测和绝经后激素方案分类中取得了最高AUC，且模型解释产生了生物学上有意义的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为预测性表示。现有通用或医学适配的语言模型缺乏代谢组学领域的专门知识，需要构建一个能整合异构生化知识并用于下游预测任务的领域专用模型。

Method: MetaboLLM通过三个阶段的适配构建：持续预训练（continual pretraining）、监督微调（supervised fine-tuning）和结构化检索（structured retrieval）。MetaboLLM-GIN则将MetaboLLM生成的生化描述转化为代谢物图，通过图同构网络（GIN）进行患者级预测。评估覆盖四个骨干模型家族，并与其他图构建方法和未适配/无检索的LLM配置进行对比。

Result: MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上优于对应的基座模型和医学适配模型，并能迁移到外部公共基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉旁路移植术后应激性高血糖预测中取得最高AUC（0.8616），在绝经后激素方案分类中取得0.8123的AUC，均优于传统模型、替代图构建方法及其他LLM配置。模型解释产生了生物学上有意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域专用语言模型能够将异构生化知识组织成可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，为代谢组学驱动的临床预测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [243] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RxnCLF（反应对比学习基础模型），一种基于自监督对比学习框架的反应表示学习方法。RxnCLF构建了冷凝反应图（CRG）统一反应物和产物信息，在170万条Pistachio反应上预训练后，通过微调在多个反应产率预测基准上显著优于现有图与序列基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标注数据稀缺、反应空间组合巨大且稀疏的问题，现有基于字符串、指纹和图的方法仅能部分捕捉化学转化信息，导致对复杂底物反应的预测不准确，亟需更好的反应表示学习方法。

Method: RxnCLF采用自监督对比学习框架，基于冷凝反应图（CRG）将反应物与产物信息统一为单一图结构，使模型学习显式且丰富的转化结构而非分离图。模型在170万条Pistachio反应上预训练，学习包含反应中心特征和侧链上下文的紧凑连续潜空间，随后在多个产率预测基准上进行微调。

Result: 在Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化的BH偶联以及专有的HTE C-N偶联和酰胺化数据集等多个产率预测基准上，RxnCLF一致优于图和序列基线方法，显著提升R2指标并获得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: CRG-based RxnCLF展示了作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力，有望泛化到更广泛的反应空间，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等多种下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [244] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应（根据先前响应选择后续测试）与非自适应（预先固定所有测试）之间的查询复杂度差异。作者证明：对两类分布，可区分当且仅当它们的成对条件概率存在正分离；当分离为零时，任何有限查询预算下的最优最坏情况误差恰为1/2。作者构造了一个使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个成对查询的随机非自适应过程，其模拟转录与自适应过程的总变差距离在ρ以内。同时构造匹配的示例族，其自适应查询复杂度为常数而ε-非自适应查询复杂度为Ω_ε(N²)，证明最坏情况固定误差的自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估中一个核心问题在于测试选择策略：是预先固定所有查询，还是根据先前响应动态调整后续查询。理解自适应带来的查询复杂度优势及界限，对于评估协议的设计和资源分配具有理论和实践意义。

Method: 在有限结果空间上的条件查询模型中，首先刻画可区分性与成对条件概率分离的关系；其次通过构造随机非自适应程序模拟自适应过程（使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个对查询），并以总变差距离为度量建立了两种查询策略之间的转录相似性；最后通过匹配的示例族建立自适应的下界。

Result: 学习成立当且仅当两类分布之间成对条件概率有正分离；零分离时最优最坏情况误差恰为1/2。非自适应模拟自适应的查询开销为O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个对查询，且自适应差距（adaptivity gap）的最坏情况为Θ_ε(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互（自适应）可以将所需测试数量减少二次因子，但交互式评估的指数级分支并不会带来指数级的查询优势。这界定了自适应评估的真实价值：有实质好处但收益有限。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [245] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，一种直接基于次梯度操作的Langevin离散化方法，用于非光滑、超线性梯度增长且非凸的目标分布采样。算法采用taming技术保证稳定性，推导出Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，并提供了相应优化问题的超额风险估计。作者在GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练势能上验证了假设，其boosted坐标变体与微调的AdamW和Muon竞争表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Langevin采样算法在处理同时具备非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的目标分布时面临挑战，通常依赖计算代价高昂的平滑化处理。已有的基于次梯度的Langevin算法收敛速率不够理想，需要一种更高效、稳定且具有更优收敛保证的方法。

Method: 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)，即直接作用于次梯度的tamed离散化Langevin扩散方案。通过taming技术处理超线性增长带来的不稳定性，构造显式且稳定的数值格式，避免使用平滑化操作。推导Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数均显式追踪维度与逆温度依赖，并给出优化问题的超额风险界。

Result: 所获得的非渐近收敛速率优于当前已知的次梯度型Langevin算法；对GPT-2系列LLM正则化预训练势能验证了所有假设（含显式常数）；提出的boosted坐标变体SG-TULA在预训练GPT-2模型时与微调后的AdamW和Muon竞争效果相当，而这些方法目前没有可比的非渐近理论保证。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为具挑战性的非凸、非光滑且超线性增长势能的Langevin采样提供了理论基础扎实且实践效果良好的新方案，其理论速率优于现有方法并在真实LLM预训练任务中展现了实际竞争力。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [246] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB框架，利用注意力机制估计生存分析中的个体治疗获益概率（IPTB），将问题转化为二元分类，借助成对患者比较和不精确概率处理右删失，在非线性合成数据集上优于多种元学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析场景中，传统方法难以直接估计个体层面的治疗获益概率（IPTB），且右删失数据的存在使得精确概率估计面临挑战。现有元学习器（T-learner、S-learner等）在复杂非线性特征空间下性能显著退化，需要一种可扩展且统计上严谨的个性化治疗获益评估方案。

Method: Surv-IPTB将IPTB估计重构为二元分类问题，利用治疗组与对照组的成对患者比较进行学习。框架通过不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原理性地处理右删失观测；引入带可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，实现成对比较的灵活数据驱动聚合，同时为删失样本学习软类别概率。

Result: 在包含螺旋形、钟形和环形等复杂非线性结构的合成数据集上，Surv-IPTB在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下均保持稳健性能。模型持续优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险和Beran估计器的T-learner和S-learner基线，尤其在传统方法显著退化的非线性场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: Surv-IPTB作为一种可扩展且统计上严谨的注意力框架，为生存场景下个性化治疗获益评估提供了有效解决方案，其代码已公开可用。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [247] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BaKron，一种用于神经网络量化的高效求解器，利用Kronecker分解的Hessian近似（双边自适应舍入），通过反对角线并行和递归分治构造，将计算复杂度从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，在保持GPTQ三次方级扩展的同时利用更丰富的曲率信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ风格的自适应舍入通常仅利用输入激活的"单边"信息，而双边Kronecker分解Hessian近似能捕捉输出坐标间的相关性，但直接在向量化权重域应用GPTQ计算成本过高。因此需要一种高效求解器来利用双边曲率信息进行量化。

Method: BaKron结合反对角线（anti-diagonal）并行与递归分治（divide-and-conquer）构造，构建在BoA和YAQA的双边自适应舍入框架之上。对于m×n权重矩阵，仅需O(m+n)顺序步骤，将总工作量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))。此外，BaKron在基础量化器和Hessian估计器方面均具有模块化特性。

Result: BaKron达到了与GPTQ相同三次方级别的计算扩展，同时利用了更丰富的Hessian曲率信息。论文提供了实际基准测试，考察了多种可用的Hessian，提出了一种高效计算这些Hessian的技术，并通过实验验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: BaKron为基于Kronecker分解Hessian的自适应量化提供了一种高效且模块化的求解方案，在计算复杂度和信息利用率之间取得了良好平衡，适用于大规模神经网络量化场景。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [248] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督在线策略自蒸馏（U-OPSD）方法，仅利用模型自身生成内容和内部一致性实现自蒸馏，无需任何外部监督信号（如真实标签、环境反馈或更大模型的指导）。U-OPSD通过多数投票构造伪解决方案，并基于最短伪解构建教师分布，蒸馏到模型最长错误补全的前缀中。在多个基准和模型规模上，U-OPSD一致超越基础模型，匹配或超越有监督方法（OPSD、GRPO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略自蒸馏（OPD/OPSD）方法虽已展现出对LLM后训练的巨大潜力，但仍然依赖外部监督信号（真实标签、环境反馈或更大模型指导），未能实现真正的"自我"蒸馏。作者希望探索仅利用模型自身生成内容实现自蒸馏的可能性，摆脱对外部监督的依赖。

Method: U-OPSD的核心流程为：（1）采样多个rollout输出；（2）通过自一致性阈值下的多数投票构造伪解决方案；（3）以最短伪解为条件构建教师分布；（4）将该教师分布蒸馏到模型最长错误补全的前缀，使模型在"确信错误"的位置精准自我纠错。整个过程不依赖任何外部监督信息。

Result: 在不同基准、基础模型和训练设置下，U-OPSD一致优于基础模型，并与使用真实标签的有监督方法（OPSD、GRPO）持平或超越。在非思考模式Qwen3的4B和8B规模上，AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500和AMC23上分别比基础模型提升8.5%和10.7%，平均超过OPSD 3.2%和2.3%。思考模式下与OPSD相当（4B超0.9%，8B持平），且分别超越GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD证明了无需任何外部监督，仅依靠模型自身生成和内部一致性即可实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏。该方法在多个基准上匹配甚至超越依赖真实标签的有监督方法，为LLM后训练提供了一条更自主、更可扩展的新路径。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [249] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RRC（Ranking-based Reward Construction）方法，解决生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的潜力未充分释放问题。RRC通过自竞争排序和锚点引导排序两种策略，从相对偏好排序中构建有效的奖励信号，在开放式对话和推理基准上显著优于现有奖励构建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型在响应排序方面表现优异，但在强化学习中未充分发挥潜力。究其原因，生成式奖励建模的比较性本质与现有RL算法采用的标量评分范式之间存在不匹配，需要设计能够利用相对偏好排序信息的奖励构建方法。

Method: 提出RRC（Ranking-based Reward Construction）方法，包含两种互补策略：1）自竞争排序（self-competitive ranking），利用采样响应之间的比较得出奖励；2）锚点引导排序（anchor-guided ranking），通过少量参考响应实现可扩展的基于排序的奖励构建。

Result: 在开放式对话和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC显著改善了使用生成式奖励模型的RL训练效果，相较于现有奖励构建方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将生成式奖励模型的比较性能力转化为基于相对偏好排序的RL学习信号，RRC有效弥合了生成式奖励建模与标量评分RL范式之间的鸿沟，释放了生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [250] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一个自主终端任务合成系统，通过验证求解器行为来修订候选任务，实现求解器相对的可学习区域校准，构建了5,431个校准任务，在多个基准上显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端代理需要可执行且可验证的任务，且这些任务不仅要可解，还要对学习具有适当挑战性。可执行性验证只能确保可行性，无法揭示任务相对于特定求解器设置的行为表现。因此需要一种方法来根据求解器的已验证行为校准任务难度。

Method: CalibForge采用两种校准策略：(1) 多求解器校准，针对异构求解器池内的不一致性；(2) 对比求解器校准，针对指定的强通过/弱失败关系。两者都基于已验证的可解性来确立求解器相对的可学习区域。通过对抗性求解器校准来修订候选任务。

Result: 构建了5,431个校准终端任务。消融实验表明两种策略比单独的作者生成加验证或普通单求解器反馈更有效。在完整集合上训练的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%，最大改进相对于对应基础模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上提升24.71个百分点，在SWE-bench Pro上提升27.68个百分点，在Doc2Repo上提升30.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对可学习性（solver-relative learnability）是构建有效且可迁移的代理训练数据的实用目标，实验结果支持该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [251] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限VC维二元假设类，构造了一个学习器，实现与统计下界（含L*依赖）匹配的最优风险界，解决了agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度问题（仅差通用常数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VC理论早已给出学习算法的通用风险界，但这些界对最优风险L*的依赖不是最优的。Devroye等人给出的下界表明风险上界中L*的平方根项是本质的，但此前没有算法能在每个固定L*下同时匹配上下界。本文旨在构造一个能同时匹配已知下界（差常数因子）的学习算法，从而明确样本复杂度。

Method: 基于有限VC维假设类，构造一个特定的学习算法，证明其风险满足含L*的平方根项与线性项的联合上界，常数因子约为7×10^8。

Result: 得到了如下风险界：对任意0<δ≤1/2，以至少1-δ概率有L(ĥ)≤L*+7×10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)。该界在任意固定L*下与Devroye等人的下界吻合（仅常数不同）。

Conclusion: 该结果在通用常数意义上解决了agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度问题，表明此前下界在结构上是紧的。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Media Meets Communication in 6G: Fundamentals, Key Technologies, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05184)
*Bingyan Xie,Longyu Zhou,Zihan Chen,Shunpu Tang,Mingyang Shi,Yu Tian,Guo Lu,Yongpeng Wu,Tianhao Liang,Tony Q. S. Quek,Guangtao Zhai,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了面向6G视觉通信的媒体通信技术，通过回顾通信与媒体技术的发展演变，提出了一个涵盖AI驱动媒体技术、媒体感知无线传输、大模型赋能媒体通信和智能网络基础设施四个关键维度的统一框架，重点探讨了语义联合信源信道优化、源感知传输优化和信道感知信源优化三个互补视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络的快速发展加速了媒体智能与通信智能的融合，传统比特级传输范式逐渐向智能、语义感知和生成式范式转变。新兴媒体服务不仅需要高数据率和低延迟，还要求语义感知、感知质量保证、自适应资源编排、可信内容处理和个性化媒体生成，迫切需要系统性梳理媒体内容处理与无线传输之间的内在关系。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，首先回顾通信技术和媒体技术的演进历程，厘清两者之间的内在关系；随后提出一个包含四个关键维度的统一框架：AI驱动媒体技术（涵盖媒体编码、内容理解、质量评估、安全合规检测和AIGC媒体生成）、媒体感知无线传输（从语义联合信源信道优化、源感知传输优化和信道感知信源优化三个互补视角展开）、大模型赋能媒体通信以及智能网络基础设施。

Result: 提出了面向6G视觉通信的媒体通信统一分析框架，系统梳理了媒体内容处理与无线传输之间的协同优化关系，明确了从比特级交付向语义感知和生成式范式演进的路径，并覆盖了媒体编码、内容理解、质量评估、安全检测、AIGC生成以及无线传输优化等全方位关键技术。

Conclusion: 6G媒体通信正从传统比特传输向智能语义感知和生成式范式变革，需要一个统一的跨学科框架来协同优化媒体内容处理与无线传输，其中AI驱动媒体技术、语义感知的无线传输优化以及大模型与智能网络基础设施的融合将是未来发展的核心方向。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of sixth-generation (6G) networks is accelerating the convergence of media intelligence and communication intelligence, driving media communication beyond conventional bit-level delivery toward intelligent, semantic-aware, and generative paradigms. Emerging media services require not only high data rates and low latency, but also semantic awareness, perceptual quality assurance, adaptive resource orchestration, trustworthy content processing, and personalized media generation. Meanwhile, media technologies are evolving from handcrafted signal processing and conventional coding toward artificial intelligence (AI)-driven representation learning, content understanding, and generative reconstruction. Motivated by these trends, this paper presents a systematic survey of media communication technologies for 6G vision communication by revisiting the evolution of communication and media technologies and clarifying the intrinsic relationship between media content processing and wireless transmission. We introduce a unified framework consisting of four key dimensions: AI-driven media technologies, media-aware wireless transmission, large model-enabled media communication, and intelligent network infrastructures. Specifically, AI-driven media technologies encompass media coding, content understanding, quality assessment, security and compliance detection, and AIGC-enabled media generation, while media-aware wireless transmission is examined from three complementary perspectives: semantic joint source-channel optimization, which jointly encodes task-relevant semantic information; source-aware transmission optimization, which leverages media characteristics for channel adaptation, prediction, and compensation; and channel-aware source optimization, which adapts media coding and reconstruction based on real-time channel conditions.

</details>


### [253] [Diversity in Coded TE-QKD Channels: Achieving Infinite Diversity out of Finite System Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05432)
*Shaikha S. Al-Qahtani,Siyao Li,Joseph J. Boutros*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文首次证明在时间纠缠量子密钥分发（TE-QKD）密钥协商中，纠错码可以实现无限分集阶数，即使信道具有有限分集且码长相对较短。这一结果在编码与通信理论文献中前所未有，因为经典衰落信道中解码只能将有限分集阶数乘以有限因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分集阶数是衡量高速信噪比下错误概率渐近斜率的关键性能指标。作者希望探究TE-QKD密钥协商这一特殊信道环境下，纠错码能否突破经典衰落信道中有限分集阶数的限制，获得无限分集增益。

Method: 对于有界距离代数解码，作者推导了以每码字光子数和解码半径为变量的充要条件；对于软判决解码，引入了最大有限分集（MFD）性质，并证明当且仅当码是MFD缺陷时才能实现无限分集。

Result: 基于Golay、Reed-Solomon、BCH和Reed-Muller码的短码实例验证了分析结果，展示了TE-QKD系统参数和相对较短的码参数如何影响硬协商与软协商的可实现分集阶数。

Conclusion: TE-QKD中的无限分集在经典衰落信道中没有对应物，揭示了量子密钥分发纠错协商中一个独特的理论现象，为短码设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: We establish conditions and give proofs on how an error-correcting code can attain infinite diversity in a time-entanglement quantum key distribution (TE-QKD) reconciliation. The shocking result, never encountered in the literature on coding and communication theory, is that a decoder exhibits an infinite diversity order while the channel has finite diversity and the code has a relatively short finite length.
  This paper studies the diversity order of coded TE-QKD reconciliation, defined by the asymptotic slope of the error probability at high signal-to-noise ratio. For bounded-distance algebraic decoding, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition in terms of the number of photons per codeword and the decoding radius.
  For soft-decision decoding, we introduce the maximal finite diversity (MFD) property and prove that infinite diversity is achieved if and only if the code is MFD deficient. The infinite diversity in TE-QKD has no counterpart in classical fading channels, where decoding can only multiply a finite diversity order by a finite factor.
  Examples of short codes based on Golay, Reed-Solomon, Bose-Chaudhuri-Hocquenghem (BCH), and Reed-Muller codes validate the analysis and illustrate how the TE-QKD system parameters and the relatively short code parameters affect the achievable diversity for both hard and soft information reconciliation.

</details>


### [254] [An Ordered-Reliability-Bits Chase Decoding Algorithm for BCH Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05591)
*Wenwu Zhu,Min Zhu,Baoming Bai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低复杂度的ORB-Chase译码算法，用于BCH码。该算法在传统Chase算法基础上做了两点改进：使用逻辑权重作为度量来生成测试错误模式（TEPs），并引入基于整数的提前终止判据。仿真表明该算法在(127,113,5)BCH码和(256,239,6)eBCH码上能以显著更少的测试模式实现接近最大似然（ML）的性能，且BM译码调用次数平均随信噪比增加而快速下降，在相同BLER性能下相比Chase算法最多减少98.1%的计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Chase译码算法虽然能逼近最大似然性能，但需要生成大量测试错误模式并进行多次BM译码，计算复杂度较高。在实际通信系统中，需要在保证译码性能（接近ML）的同时降低计算复杂度，以满足低功耗、高吞吐量的需求，因此有必要对Chase算法进行改进以降低其计算开销。

Method: 提出ORB-Chase译码算法，核心改进包括：(1) 采用逻辑权重（logical weight）作为度量标准来生成测试错误模式（TEPs），替代传统Chase算法中基于可靠性排序的方式；(2) 引入基于整数的提前终止判据，一旦识别出最大似然码字即可在最早期阶段停止计算，避免不必要的BM译码调用。通过(127,113,5)BCH码和(256,239,6)eBCH码的仿真来验证算法性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，ORB-Chase算法在(127,113,5)BCH码和(256,239,6)eBCH码上能够以显著更少的测试模式实现接近最大似然（near-ML）的译码性能。此外，平均BM译码调用次数随E_b/N_0的增加而快速下降，在相同BLER性能下相比传统Chase算法最多可减少98.1%的BM译码调用。

Conclusion: ORB-Chase算法通过逻辑权重度量生成测试错误模式和整数型提前终止判据，在保持接近ML译码性能的同时大幅降低了计算复杂度，尤其在高信噪比区域计算量节省尤为显著，是一种适合BCH/eBCH码的高效低复杂度译码方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a low-complexity ordered-reliability-bits Chase (ORB-Chase) decoding algorithm for BCH codes. The proposed algorithm differs from the traditional Chase algorithm in two key aspects. First, it employs the logical weight as a metric to generate test error patterns (TEPs). Second, it introduces an integer-based early termination criterion that ensures computation can stop at the earliest possible stage if the maximum-likelihood codeword is identified, thereby minimizing unnecessary computational effort. Simulation results for (127, 113, 5) BCH codes and (256, 239, 6) eBCH codes demonstrate that the ORB-Chase algorithm achieves near-ML performance with significantly fewer test patterns compared to the Chase algorithm. Moreover, the average number of Berlekamp-Massey (BM) decoding calls decreases rapidly as $E_b / N_0$ increases, achieving a reduction of up to 98.1% compared to the Chase algorithm at the same BLER performance.

</details>


### [255] [Maximal achievable service rates of some classes of linear codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05657)
*Priyanka Choudhary,Monika Yadav,Maheshanand Bhaintwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于组合结构（t-设计、差集和BIBD）构造的线性码（包括循环码和LDPC码）中数据符号的最大可达服务速率的下界。分别在系统线性码和非系统二元码两种情形下建立了下界，分析了BIBD-LDPC码、扩展系统线性码以及由差集构造的循环码，并给出了Singer差集循环码每个数据符号最大可达服务速率的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性码中数据符号的最大可达服务速率下界，特别是利用组合结构（如t-设计、差集、BIBD）来推导这些下界，以期为通信系统中数据符号的服务速率提供理论保证。

Method: (i) 在C⊥中固定重量码字支撑构成BIBD的条件下，建立系统线性码C的最大可达服务速率下界；(ii) 在C⊥中固定重量码字支撑构成t-设计的条件下，建立非系统二元码的下界。利用BIBD关联矩阵构造的线性码参数推导服务速率下界，分析扩展系统线性码，利用SBIBD推导系统循环码下界，并对由差集构造的循环码族给出显式下界。

Result: 建立了系统线性码和非系统二元码的最大可达服务速率下界；展示了BIBD参数如何用于推导LDPC码的服务速率下界；证明了SBIBD的存在可推导系统循环码的下界；给出了差集构造的循环码的显式下界；确定了由Singer差集构造的循环码每个数据符号最大可达服务速率的精确值。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地建立了线性码数据符号最大可达服务速率的组合下界方法，并针对多类码（BIBD-LDPC码、循环码、扩展线性码）给出了相应的下界，特别对Singer差集循环码实现了精确值的确定。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate lower bounds on the maximum achievable service rates for data symbols in certain classes of linear codes, including cyclic codes and low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes, that are derived from combinatorial structures such as $t$-designs, difference sets, and balanced incomplete block designs (BIBDs). We first establish a lower bound on the maximum achievable service rate for each data symbol in the following two cases: (i) systematic linear codes $C$ under the assumption that the supports of codewords of a fixed weight in $C^\perp$ form a BIBD, and (ii) non-systematic binary codes under the assumption that the supports of codewords of a fixed weight in $C^\perp$ form a $t$-design. We then investigate the linear codes obtained from the incidence matrices of BIBDs, particularly certain classes of BIBD-LDPC codes, and show how the parameters of the underlying designs can be exploited to determine lower bounds on the maximum achievable service rates for the data symbols of the corresponding linear code. In addition, we analyze the maximum achievable service rates of systematic extended linear codes. We show that the existence of a symmetric BIBD (SBIBD) corresponding to a dual codeword can be used to derive a lower bound on the maximum achievable service rate of the associated systematic cyclic code. We also present some families of cyclic codes constructed from difference sets and obtain explicit lower bounds on the maximum achievable service rates for their data symbols. In particular, we determine the exact values of the maximum achievable service rates for each data symbol of cyclic codes arising from Singer difference sets.

</details>


### [256] [Wireless Linear Computation Broadcast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05692)
*Shuo Tan,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出无线线性计算广播（WLCBC）框架，将线性计算广播问题推广到高斯MIMO广播信道，考虑噪声接收端侧信息、发射功率约束和任意天线配置，并设计了基于加权和均方误差最小化的联合线性收发机交替优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LCBC研究假设无噪声理想广播信道，而实际无线广播信道存在噪声和功率限制。需要将LCBC推广到无线环境中，以处理噪声侧信息和信道退化带来的挑战。

Method: 将问题建模为加权和MSE最小化问题。对固定预编码器，最优解码器是LMMSE估计器；对固定解码器，预编码器更新转化为凸的QCQP问题，可由单个对偶变量参数化得到半闭式解。采用交替优化算法，保证目标函数单调递减。

Result: 所提交替优化算法保证加权和MSE目标的单调下降和收敛性。仿真结果表明，相比基于先前工作的自然基线方法，所提方案具有显著的鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为LCBC在无线物理层的实际应用提供了可行的收发机设计框架，验证了在噪声无线信道中实现高效分布式线性计算广播的可行性，并展现了相对于现有方法的性能优势。

Abstract: A linear computation broadcast (LCBC) problem comprises $K$ users (receivers) and a transmitter. The users wish to compute various (vector) linear functions of a common dataset, and possess in advance heterogeneous side information corresponding to various other linear computations. The goal for the broadcast transmitter, who knows the dataset and all desired and side-information functions, is to satisfy all demands as efficiently as possible. Prior work has explored the information-theoretic capacity of LCBC for zero-error finite-field computation over an ideal (noiseless) broadcast channel (BC). This paper develops a wireless LCBC (WLCBC) framework for the Gaussian MIMO BC with noisy receiver-side information under a transmit power constraint and arbitrary antenna configurations. In the WLCBC setting, a Gaussian source is linearly precoded for broadcast; each user requests a linear function of the source and forms an estimate by linearly combining its channel observation with its noisy side information. Assuming perfect channel state information, we cast the centralized joint linear transceiver design as a weighted sum-MSE minimization problem and propose an efficient alternating optimization algorithm. For a fixed precoder, the optimal decoders are the linear minimum mean-square error (LMMSE) estimators. For fixed decoders, the precoder update reduces to a convex quadratically constrained quadratic program which leads to a semi-closed-form solution parameterized by a single dual variable. The resulting algorithm guarantees a monotonic decrease in the objective and convergence of the weighted sum-MSE (WSMSE) objective sequence. Simulations demonstrate pronounced robustness gains over natural baselines obtained from prior works.

</details>


### [257] [Moment-based linear programming bounds for locally recoverable codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05758)
*Shujian Li,Hengjia Wei,Maosheng Xiong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为q元(r,δ)-局部可恢复码(LRCs)推导了新的Delsarte型线性规划(LP)界，该LP具有三个特点：变量集大小接近经典Delsarte LP；利用局部距离条件通过阶δ-2施加的高阶信息；适用于一般线性与非线性(r,δ)-LRCs。计算显示该LP产生的凸包界优于已有LP，并常能改进缩短界和广义Singleton界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有LP界在LRC问题上存在局限，需要一种既能利用高阶局部距离信息、变量规模又与经典Delsarte LP相当、且适用于线性和非线性码的更强的LP界。

Method: 推导新的Delsarte型线性规划界，其变量集规模与经典Delsarte LP相仿；通过阶δ-2利用局部距离条件带来的高阶信息（平衡基部分与对称化精化权重LP等价，额外约束在δ≥3时非平凡）；适用于一般(r,δ)-LRCs（含线性和非线性）。

Result: 在二元和三元字母表上的大量计算表明，凸包LP界优于此前LP界，并常常能够改进缩短界和广义Singleton界。

Conclusion: 所提出的新LP界为(r,δ)-LRCs提供了更紧的界，尤其在高阶局部距离信息利用和非线性情形下具有优势。

Abstract: In this paper we derive new Delsarte-type linear programming bounds for $q$-ary $(r,δ)$-locally recoverable codes (LRCs) with three attributes: first, the variable set is comparable in size to that of the classical Delsarte LP; second, our LP exploits the higher-order information forced by the local-distance condition through order \(δ-2\), in the sense that for nondegenerate linear codes, its balanced base part gives exactly the same dimension bound as the symmetrized refined-weight LP of Gruica, Jany, and Ravagnani, while the additional constraints, nonvacuous whenever $δ\ge 3$, give a further strengthening; and third, it applies to general $(r,δ)$-LRCs, linear and nonlinear alike. Extensive computations over binary and ternary alphabets show that the convex-hull LP yields improvements not captured by the previous LP and often sharpens the shortening and generalized Singleton bounds.

</details>


### [258] [Chernoff-Stein-Type Exponent in Testing Between Two Outlier Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05933)
*Ligong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在$2^{nR}$条长度为$n$的随机序列中，存在一条索引随机的离群序列的二元假设检验问题。在$\mathcal{H}_0$下离群序列各分量服从$Q_0$的独立同分布，在$\mathcal{H}_1$下服从$Q_1$的独立同分布，其余$2^{nR}-1$条序列在两种假设下均服从$P$的独立同分布。在$\mathcal{H}_0$下错误概率有界于1的约束下，给出了$\mathcal{H}_1$下错误概率的最快指数衰减速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的动机在于刻画在存在一条随机索引离群序列的多序列观测场景下进行二元假设检验时的极限性能。此类模型广泛应用于信号检测、网络异常检测、认知无线电等领域，其中需要从未知来源的众多候选序列中判定是否存在特定分布模式。确定在给定$\mathcal{H}_0$错误概率约束下的最大指数衰减速率，是理解此类检测问题基本极限的关键。

Method: 本文采用大偏差理论与假设检验的信息论方法，通过分析离群序列出现在随机位置这一结构特征，结合两类错误概率之间的权衡约束（$\mathcal{H}_0$下错误概率须有界远离1），推导出$\mathcal{H}_1$下错误概率的可达与不可达指数界，从而精确确定最快的指数衰减速率。

Result: 在$\mathcal{H}_0$错误概率被限制有界远离1的条件下，本文求得了$\mathcal{H}_1$下决策错误概率所能达到的最快指数衰减速率。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在含随机索引离群序列的多序列假设检验问题中，存在明确的指数级最优检测性能极限，这一极限取决于序列数量指数$R$以及各假设下分布$Q_0$、$Q_1$与背景分布$P$之间的统计特性关系。

Abstract: Among $2^{nR}$ length-$n$ random sequences, one sequence is an outlier whose index is random. Under hypothesis $\mathcal{H}_0$, the components of the outlier are independent and identically distributed (IID) according to $Q_0$, whereas under hypothesis $\mathcal{H}_1$ they are IID according to $Q_1$. The remaining $(2^{nR}-1)$ sequences are mutually independent, independent of the outlier, and IID according to $P$ under both hypotheses. Based on the observation of all $2^{nR}$ sequences, one wishes to decide between $\mathcal{H}_0$ and $\mathcal{H}_1$. Under the constraint that the decision error probability under $\mathcal{H}_0$ must be bounded away from $1$, we determine the fastest exponential decay rate of the decision error probability under $\mathcal{H}_1$.

</details>


### [259] [A Session Interaction Framework for The Multiple-Unicast Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06042)
*Sirui Liu,Zongpeng Li,Xiying Fan,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个会话交互框架，将多播猜想（undirected网络中网络编码相对路由无吞吐优势）归约为一个核心等价命题：猜想普遍成立当且仅当每个不可约核都是独立的。该框架通过两阶段过程（基于会话支配的简化条件和会话解耦定理）系统性地验证该猜想在一般网络拓扑中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多播猜想是网络编码领域的核心开放问题，其成立与否直接关系到计算复杂度的基本下界。当前该猜想的验证缺乏系统性方法，需要一种能将全局可行性问题分解为可操作子问题的框架。

Method: 提出Session Interaction Framework，包含三个核心组件：(1) 核心等价归约——将猜想归结为"每个不可约核是否独立"；(2) 会话支配的简化充分条件——提供几何准则迭代简化复杂会话集；(3) 会话解耦定理——将猜想对会话集的验证归结为其独立子集。拓扑上证明被高成本割或割点分离的会话保证独立。

Result: 证明了多播猜想普遍成立等价于每个不可约核是独立的；提出了会话支配的简化充分条件（几何准则）；证明了会话解耦定理，将被高成本割或割点分离的会话确认为独立；整合成一个两阶段的系统性验证方法论。

Conclusion: 通过归约、简化和解耦三种机制的综合集成，该框架为跨一般网络拓扑验证多播猜想提供了一套系统的、可操作的方法论，将全局可行性问题转化为对不可约核心独立性的局部分解检查。

Abstract: The multiple-unicast conjecture asserts that network coding offers no throughput advantage over routing in undirected networks. Its validity is known to imply fundamental lower bounds in computational complexity. We propose a Session Interaction Framework that reduces the conjecture to a central equivalence: the conjecture holds universally if and only if every irreducible core is independent. This result transforms the global feasibility problem into a two-stage process. First, to make the reduction phase tractable, we provide simplified sufficient conditions for session dominance, offering geometric criteria to iteratively simplify complex session sets. Second, for the remaining "irreducible core," we propose a Session Decoupling Theorem, reducing the conjecture's validity for a session set to its independent subsets. Topologically, we prove that sessions separated by high-cost cuts or cut-vertices are guaranteed to be independent. By integrating these reduction and decomposition mechanisms, our framework offers a systematic methodology to verify the conjecture across general network topologies.

</details>


### [260] [On the Multiple-Unicast Conjecture: Beyond Cut Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06070)
*Sirui Liu,Li Que,Zongpeng Li,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对网络编码领域著名的多播猜想（multiple-unicast conjecture），提出了一个统一的度量框架，将编码与路由对比的基本对象从割集扩展到图度量。利用该框架，作者证明了该猜想在多个新网络类别上成立，包括至多五个终端位置的网络、终端位置位于至多三个指定面边界上的平面网络、以及满足某种结构限制的大规模网络。此外，作者还给出了无需计算机辅助搜索的、关于至多六个编码节点网络的新证明，并建立了与Γ₃,₃图相关的条件性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络编码允许多播传输中的中间节点对消息编码后再转发，而多播猜想断言在无向网络中，对于独立单播会话，编码相比分数路由（fractional routing）没有吞吐量优势。尽管该猜想被研究了二十多年，仍是核心未解难题。该猜想与计算复杂性理论深度关联——若能证明，将带来许多基础问题长期追寻的下界。因此，研究此猜想具有重要的理论与计算复杂性意义。

Method: 本文提出了一个统一的度量框架（metric framework），核心理念是：比较编码与路由时，基本对象不应仅仅是割（cuts），而应是图度量（graph metrics）。基于这一框架，作者据此对三类新无向网络证明了多播猜想，包括：(a) 至多五个终端位置的网络；(b) 终端位置位于至多三个指定面边界上、且每个会话的端点在同一边界面上的平面网络；(c) 在会话端点满足特定结构限制下、节点和终端位置数量任意的网络。此外，作者给出了无需计算机辅助搜索的、针对至多六个编码节点网络的新证明，并证明了条件性结果：若猜想在Γ₃,₃上成立，则当任意三个会话不具有六个不同终端位置时，猜想亦成立。

Result: 本文通过统一度量框架，成功将多播猜想证明扩展至三类新的无向网络类别：(a) 至多五个终端位置的网络；(b) 满足特定面边界条件的平面网络；(c) 受结构限制的任意规模网络。同时给出了至多六个编码节点网络的无计算机制证明，以及基于Γ₃,₃的归约性结果，为这一长期未解的核心猜想提供了重要进展。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入图度量视角的统一定义，为多播猜想的研究提供了新的理论工具，并在多类新网络上取得了证明性突破。这些结果不仅推进了多播猜想本身的解决，也为网络编码与路由的对比研究以及相关计算复杂性下界的推导奠定了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: Network coding allows intermediate nodes to encode received messages before transmission. The multiple-unicast conjecture asserts that coding has no throughput advantage over fractional routing for independent unicast sessions in any undirected network. Despite more than two decades of sustained study, this central open problem remains unresolved. The conjecture is deeply connected to computational complexity: a proof would yield long-sought lower bounds for fundamental problems. To study the conjecture, this paper develops a unified metric framework from the perspective that the basic objects behind the comparison between coding and routing are not cuts alone, but graph metrics. Using this framework, we prove the conjecture for three new classes of undirected networks: (a) networks with at most five terminal locations; (b) planar networks whose terminal locations lie on the boundaries of at most three designated faces, with each session's endpoints on one such face; and (c) networks with arbitrarily many nodes and terminal locations under a structural restriction on session endpoints. We give a new proof that the conjecture holds for networks with at most six coding nodes, without computer-aided search, and show that if the conjecture holds on $Γ_{3,3}$, then it holds whenever no three sessions have six distinct terminal locations.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Discrete energy as an exact label-free training objective for finite-element surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05437)
*Ruifeng Cao,Xidan Song*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考解（label-free）的有限元代理模型训练方法：利用离散势能在线性静弹性问题中作为训练信号。理论证明预测能量与参考能量之差等于刚度范数平方误差的一半，且能量梯度等于刚度加权误差，从而标签自由离散能量最小化与刚度范数监督回归具有相同的最优点和梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元代理模型的监督训练需要参考解，而每个参考解都必须求解代理模型本应替代的线性系统，这构成了一个计算瓶颈。本文旨在寻找一种无需参考解的训练信号，打破这一依赖。

Method: 以组装后的离散势能作为无标签训练信号，推导其精确的数学性质：能量差与平方刚度范数误差的关系、能量梯度与刚度加权误差的关系；辅以条件分析引理、模态收缩恒等式、Chebyshev界及JEPA联合嵌入架构的条件分离命题。所有含数值内容的命题均以可执行的伪证检查实现。

Result: 所有理论命题的数值不等式均在合成测试问题和16个预注册验证集实例上通过，并报告了紧度度量；同时给出了一个数值反例界定条件分离命题的适用范围。此外证明直接最小化作用泛函无法将构造推广至弹性动力学，但给出了恢复精确性的时间离散公式。

Conclusion: 标签自由的离散能量最小化与刚度范数监督回归在线性静弹性代理模型训练中严格等价，提供了无需参考解的可行训练范式；其推广到弹性动力学需采用特定的时间离散化方法。

Abstract: Supervised training of finite-element (FE) surrogate models requires reference solutions, and each reference solution is obtained by solving the system that the surrogate is intended to replace. The assembled discrete potential energy provides a training signal that requires no reference solution. This note records, with proofs, the identities that make this signal exact for linear elastostatics: the difference between the energy of a prediction and the energy of the reference solution equals one half of the squared stiffness-norm error, and the gradient of the energy equals the stiffness-weighted error. Label-free discrete-energy minimisation and supervised regression in the stiffness norm therefore have the same unique minimiser and identical gradients at every point. Around this central result, the note states a conditioning lemma that bounds the displacement error by the energy gap, a modewise contraction identity that explains why the Euclidean displacement error is an unsuitable primary metric, the Chebyshev bound that governs conjugate-gradient post-processing of surrogate predictions, and a conditional latent-separation proposition for joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) pretraining on a shared stiffness operator, with an explicit numerical counterexample that delimits its scope. Every claim with numeric content is implemented as an executable falsification check; the checks were executed twice, on synthetic test problems and on a probe set of 16 instances from the validation split of a pre-registered experimental run, and every inequality holds, with the measured tightness reported. A closing section explains why the construction does not extend to elastodynamics through direct minimisation of the action functional, and which time-discrete formulation restores exactness.

</details>


### [262] [Variable-Horizon Workforce Demand Forecasting with an Aggregate Demand Constraint for Construction Workforce Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05551)
*Hanbyeol park,Jaehyeon Heo,Taekhyun Park,Minseong Kim,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出约束保留残差分配预测（CP-RAF）方法，用于解决建筑施工中劳动力需求预测的两个关键操作问题：可变预测期限和聚合需求约束。该方法通过系数向量表示观测需求时间序列，检索相似形状的已完成任务，采用相似度加权平均估计剩余任务时长的分配曲线，并按预定义剩余劳动力需求分配，同时保留曲线特征。实验表明CP-RAF在中等和长期固定长度预测中优于八种基线模型，且在变长预测下保持较低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑施工中劳动力规划需要准确预测各任务的未来劳动力需求，但现有ML预测模型（1）假设固定长度输出，无法适应任务完成日期不同导致的可变预测期限；（2）未显式施加聚合需求约束，无法保证预测的每日需求总和等于预先指定的总劳动力分配。这两个操作要求使现有模型不适用。

Method: CP-RAF分三步：(1) 将观测的劳动力需求时间序列表示为系数向量，检索时间形状相似的历史已完成任务；(2) 利用相似度加权平均估计剩余任务时长的分配曲线（allocation profile）；(3) 按估计的分配曲线将每个任务预定义的剩余劳动力需求进行分配，并调整预测期限以保留曲线特征。该流程同时支持可变预测期限和聚合需求约束。

Result: 使用劳动力需求现场数据进行评估，CP-RAF在中等和长期固定长度预测中优于八种基线模型；在可变长度预测条件下保持了较低的预测误差。

Conclusion: 通过将操作约束直接纳入预测流程，CP-RAF为建筑施工中的劳动力分配提供了适用的预测框架，弥补了现有ML模型在操作约束适配方面的不足。

Abstract: Workforce planning is a recurring operational decision during construction projects that requires accurate forecasts of the future workforce demand for individual tasks. However, in practice, tasks have different completion dates, resulting in variable forecast horizons. In addition, the sum of the predicted daily workforce demands must equal the total workforce allocation specified in advance. Most existing machine-learning (ML)-based forecasting models assume fixed-length outputs and do not explicitly impose an aggregate demand constraint, making them unsuitable for these operational requirements. To address this problem, this study proposes constraint-preserving residual allocation forecasting (CP-RAF). The CP-RAF represents an observed workforce demand time series as a coefficient vector and retrieves completed tasks with similar temporal shapes. Then, it estimates the allocation profile over the remaining task duration using similarity-weight averaging. The predefined remaining workforce demand for each task was distributed according to the estimated profile, and the forecast horizon was adjusted while retaining profile characteristics. This procedure accommodates variable forecast horizons while preserving the aggregate demand constraints. CP-RAF was evaluated using workforce demand field data. The results showed that CP-RAF outperformed eight baseline models in medium- and long-horizon fixed-length forecasting and maintained low forecast errors under variable-length forecasting. By directly incorporating operational constraints into the forecasting procedure, the proposed method provides a framework suitable for workforce allocation in construction practices.

</details>


### [263] [A Label-Free Physics-to-Data Acceleration Framework for Parametric Time-Dependent PDEs with Latent-Space Differential-Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05554)
*Hongjiang Wang,Weizhe Wang,Yingzheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出PHD-SF，一个统一的深度算子网络（DON）框架，通过SD-TPD分离策略和微分算子学习，将物理信息求解器与数据驱动代理融合到从物理到数据的紧凑频谱中，支持三种工作模式（PIDON、HIDON、DIDON）实现无标签跨参数加速求解时变参数化偏微分方程，大幅降低对高保真数据集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速时变参数化PDE的深度学习方法从物理一致但计算昂贵的物理信息求解器到推理高效但依赖高保真数据的数据驱动代理，呈现出孤立发展的态势，仅通过显式解场标签相连。缺乏一个统一框架来复用模型信息（空间特征、潜动力学特征、空间微分算子），并减少对大规模高保真数据生成及其离线成本的依赖。

Method: 提出PHD-SF框架，核心包括：(1) SD-TPD（空间-动力学时变参数化分离）策略；(2) 微分算子学习机制；(3) 基于统一DON架构的三种工作模式：PIDON（物理信息模式）、HIDON（超约简式富集模式）、DIDON（直接推理模式）。模型信息（空间特征、潜动力学特征、空间微分算子）在三种模式间生成、继承和富集，实现无标签频谱建模，无需预计算全场解标签或显式标签传递。

Result: 在四个基准时变PDE上的实验表明，PHD-SF在初始物理信息阶段仅使用一两个代表性参数案例即可实现准确的跨参数求解和直接推理。总端到端成本低于训练单个参数案例的常规PINN，同时支持可复用的跨参数推理。

Conclusion: PHD-SF通过物理到数据的频谱框架为多查询参数化时变PDE提供了高效求解路径，显著降低了对大规模高保真数据生成及其离线成本的依赖，在保证精度的同时实现了低成本的跨参数可复用推理。

Abstract: Efficient solution of time-dependent parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is central to computational science and engineering. Existing deep-learning-based accelerators span a physics-to-data spectrum, from physics-informed solvers with strong physical consistency but high computational cost to data-driven surrogates with efficient inference but strong dependence on high-fidelity datasets. These methods have largely evolved in isolation and are often connected only through explicit solution-field labels. We propose PHD-SF, a compact physics-to-data spectrum framework for accelerating time-dependent parametric PDEs. By combining an SD-TPD separation strategy with differential-operator learning, PHD-SF allows reusable model information, including spatial features, latent dynamical features, and spatial differential operators, to be generated, inherited, and enriched across three operating modes, PIDON, HIDON, and DIDON, within a unified DON-based architecture. This design enables label-free spectrum modeling without precomputed full-field solution labels or explicit label transfer among modes. PHD-SF further establishes a separated-solving, hyper-reduction-like enrichment, and direct-inference acceleration path, reducing the dependence on large-scale high-fidelity data generation and its offline cost. Results on four benchmark time-dependent PDEs show accurate cross-parameter solution and direct inference using only one or two representative parameter cases in the initial physics-informed stage. The total end-to-end cost is lower than that required to train a conventional PINN for a single parameter case, while supporting reusable cross-parameter inference. PHD-SF therefore provides an efficient solution path for many-query parametric PDEs.

</details>


### [264] [A hybrid s-version isogeometric strategy for dynamic crack propagation in 2D and 3D problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05589)
*Tianyu He,Kosei Kurosaki,Naoki Morita,Naoto Mitsume,Kazuki Shibanuma*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合s-版本等几何分析（hS-IGA）策略，用于动态裂纹扩展分析中近裂纹断裂量的准确高效评估。该策略保留传统s-方法的全局-局部叠加框架，同时在全局离散化中引入B样条基函数，在裂纹域保留基于Lagrange的局部网格，消除了耦合积分中的不连续性，大幅减少了积分点数量（二维基准约81%，三维基准约95.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Lagrange的s-方法在全局-局部耦合积分中存在连续性相关的瓶颈，导致耦合积分需要递归细分，计算效率低下。同时裂纹表征和动态应力强度因子（DSIF）及局部应力的后处理需要保留Lagrange基的局部网格，因此需要在保持局部网格的前提下改进全局离散化以消除耦合积分的连续性障碍。

Method: 提出混合s-版本等几何分析（hS-IGA）策略：保留s-方法的全局-局部叠加框架，在全局离散化中采用B样条基函数，同时在裂纹域保留Lagrange基的局部网格。这种混合公式消除了由全局近似引起的耦合积分被积函数中的不连续性，使得标准高斯求积即可获得准确的耦合积分，无需递归细分。

Result: 通过二维静止和动态直裂纹问题（与标准有限元法及传统s-方法对比）以及三维静止和动态扩展圆形裂纹问题（与传统s-方法对比）验证。结果显示：hS-IGA策略能准确评估DSIF和局部应力，同时保留s-方法的全局-局部建模优势；耦合积分所需积分点数量大幅减少，二维动态基准减少了约81%，三维动态基准减少了约95.6%。

Conclusion: 提出的hS-IGA框架为需要可靠评估近裂纹断裂量的动态裂纹扩展分析提供了一种准确且高效的全局-局部策略，兼具s-方法的建模灵活性与等几何分析的高阶光滑性优势。

Abstract: A hybrid s-version of isogeometric analysis (hS-IGA) strategy is proposed for accurate and efficient evaluation of near-crack fracture quantities in dynamic crack propagation analysis. The strategy retains the global-local superposition framework of the conventional s-method, while introducing B-spline basis functions only into the global discretisation and preserving a Lagrange-based local mesh in the crack domain. This hybrid formulation is motivated by the continuity-related bottleneck in the global-local coupling integration of the conventional Lagrange-based s-method, and by the need to retain a Lagrange-based local mesh for crack representation and post-processing of the dynamic stress intensity factor (DSIF) and local stress. The resulting formulation removes discontinuities in the coupling integrands caused by the global approximation and enables accurate coupling integration by standard Gauss quadrature without recursive subdivision. The proposed strategy is verified using two-dimensional stationary and dynamic straight-crack problems against the standard finite element method and the conventional s-method, and is further assessed using three-dimensional stationary and dynamically propagating circular-crack problems against the conventional s-method. Results show that the proposed hS-IGA strategy accurately evaluates the DSIF and local stress while retaining the global-local modelling advantages of the s-method. It also substantially reduces the number of integration points required for coupling integration, by approximately 81% in the two-dimensional dynamic benchmark and 95.6% in the three-dimensional dynamic benchmark relative to the conventional s-method. These results demonstrate that the proposed hS-IGA framework provides an accurate and efficient global-local strategy for dynamic crack propagation analyses requiring reliable evaluation of near-crack fracture quantities.

</details>


### [265] [Benefits of Shifting Passenger Traffic from Air to Rail: A Case Study of California High-Speed Rail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05636)
*Kaijing Ding,Lu Dai,Mark Hansen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从航班延误成本降低角度量化乘客从航空转向高铁收益的方法，估计加州高铁计划在2029年和2033年分别可带来5100万-8800万美元和2.35亿-3.92亿美元（2018年美元）的航班延误成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于高铁替代航空的研究多关注旅行时间节省、票价竞争或碳排放等维度，但较少从航班延误成本的系统性视角量化高铁分流对航空网络延误缓解的收益，因此需要一套可操作的方法来评估这一收益。

Method: 首先基于加州高铁2020年商业计划中的客流预测，估算各机场起降对的航班削减数量，并将航班削减分配到每15分钟的时间段；随后使用Lasso模型估计SFO、LAX和SAN排队延误减少对全国Core 29机场到达延误的影响；最后利用飞机运营成本和乘客时间价值将延误减少货币化，并评估多种机场容量、航班时刻情景及高铁客流预测百分位情景。

Result: 在2029年航班延误成本节约估计为5100万-8800万美元（2018年美元），2033年为2.35亿-3.92亿美元（2018年美元）。

Conclusion: 高铁分流航空旅客能够在全国范围内产生可观的航班延误成本节约，这一收益应被纳入高铁项目效益评估中，且该收益对机场容量、航班时刻和高铁客流预测等情景假设较为敏感。

Abstract: This study provides a method to quantify the benefits of shifting passenger traffic from air to high-speed rail from the perspective of flight-delay cost reduction. We first estimate the number of flight reductions for airport origin-destination pairs based on the high-speed rail ridership forecasts provided in the California High-Speed Rail 2020 Business Plan, and then distribute these flight reductions to quarter-hour intervals. Lasso models are applied to estimate the impact of reduced queuing delays at SFO, LAX, and SAN on arrival delays at the national Core 29 airports. These delay reductions are then monetized using aircraft operating costs and the value of passenger time. We evaluate alternative airport-capacity and flight-schedule scenarios, as well as multiple percentiles of probabilistic high-speed rail ridership forecasts. The resulting estimates indicate flight-delay cost savings of $51-88 million in 2018 dollars in 2029 and $235-392 million in 2018 dollars in 2033.

</details>


### [266] [Hyperelastic Membranes with Implicitly Defined, Continuously Embedded Fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05717)
*Michael Wolfgang Kaiser,Thomas-Peter Fries*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于各向异性、超弹性曲面膜结构的新型力学模型及对应的有限元方法。通过嵌入超弹性纤维并采用切向微分微积分（坐标无关描述），实现隐式纤维几何与膜结构力学的高阶保真耦合数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物组织和纺织品等膜结构常表现出各向异性力学行为（由嵌入纤维引起），而现有模型在处理曲面膜结构中的纤维几何描述和力学耦合方面存在不足，需要一种基于连续介质力学第一原理、能精确描述隐式纤维几何并实现高阶数值收敛的建模与计算方法。

Method: 基于有限应变理论建立几何非线性力学模型，将超弹性纤维嵌入各向同性膜中；利用切向微分微积分框架以坐标无关方式表述微分算子，通过标量函数的水平集与膜表面的交集实现纤维几何的（半）隐式描述；纤维力学与膜力学耦合；数值上采用混合经典表面有限元与虚拟区域方法的有限元格式进行求解。

Result: 对于光滑物理场，所提数值方法获得了高阶收敛率，验证了数值方法的成功与有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为各向异性超弹性曲面膜结构提供了新颖且有效的力学模型与数值求解框架，兼具理论严谨性（基于连续介质力学第一原理）与数值精度（高阶收敛），适用于生物组织和纺织品等应用场景。

Abstract: A novel mechanical model and corresponding finite element method for anisotropic, hyperelastic, curved membranes are proposed. Hyperelastic fibers are embedded into the otherwise isotropic membrane, being relevant, for example, in reduced models for biological tissues and textiles. The geometrically nonlinear mechanics is formulated based on first principles of continuum mechanics (finite strain theory). The employed differential operators are formulated in a coordinate-free manner, through a framework known as tangential differential calculus. This enables a (semi-)implicit description of the fiber geometry through the intersection of level sets of some scalar function with the explicitly defined membrane surface. The mechanical model of the implicit fibers is then coupled to the mechanics of the membrane. For the numerical analysis, finite elements are applied such that the resulting scheme is a hybrid between classical Surface FEM and fictitious domain methods. For smooth physical fields, higher-order convergence rates are obtained and confirm the success of the numerical method.

</details>


### [267] [Accurate simulation of delamination with a resin-rich layer-dependent penalty stiffness based on structural cohesive elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05881)
*Xiaopeng Ai,Christos Kassapoglou,Boyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文将作者先前开发的高阶结构内聚单元增强为树脂富集层相关的罚刚度公式，区分法向与剪切罚刚度，并将层间树脂层罚刚度从层间框架推广到等效单层框架（通过梁理论推导的面外法向和横向剪切应力沿厚度分布实现）。与常规公式相比，该方法显著改善网格收敛性，更准确地预测裂尖前方压缩分布和分层扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壳基内聚单元因罚刚度近似而在裂尖前方过高估计压缩应力，影响预测精度；此外计算效率和网格收敛性也有待提升。

Method: 在作者先前开发的高阶结构内聚单元基础上，引入依赖树脂富集层的罚刚度改进公式。该公式区分法向与剪切罚刚度，并将基于树脂富集层的罚刚度从层间（layer-wise）框架扩展到等效单层（equivalent single-layer）框架，利用由梁理论导出的面外法向和横向剪切应力沿厚度的分布实现这一扩展。

Result: 针对Mode I、Mode II、混合模式及增强DCB构型的基准问题进行了验证，结果显示相比常规公式，该方法显著改善了网格收敛性，并能更准确地预测裂尖前方的压缩分布和分层扩展行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的树脂富集层相关罚刚度增强的高阶结构内聚单元方法在计算效率和预测精度两方面均优于常规公式，为分层扩展模拟提供了更可靠的数值工具。

Abstract: Shell-based cohesive elements tend to overestimate the compression ahead of the crack tip because of approximations in the penalty stiffness. In this study, the higher-order structural cohesive element previously developed by the authors is enhanced with a resin-rich layer-dependent penalty stiffness to improve both computational efficiency and predictive accuracy. The proposed formulation distinguishes between the normal and shear penalty stiffnesses. It extends the resin-rich layer-based penalty stiffness from the layer-wise to the equivalent single-layer framework. This extension is achieved using the through-thickness distributions of the out-of-plane normal and transverse shear stresses derived from beam theory. The proposed method is verified and validated against benchmark problems for Mode I, Mode II, mixed-mode, and reinforced DCB configurations. Compared with the conventional formulation, it exhibits significantly improved mesh convergence and provides more accurate predictions of the compression distribution ahead of the crack tip and the delamination propagation.

</details>


### [268] [Quantitative Analysis of Media Bias and Stock Price Dynamics: The 2020 Shock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05899)
*Shivansh Verma,Soham Tulsyan,Sashwat Dhanuka,Anirban Sen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文利用26家美国大型企业2015-2025年间628万条新闻标题数据，通过面板回归和带数据驱动结构断点的向量自回归模型，研究COVID-19疫情冲击前后金融新闻与个股收益率关系的动态变化，发现媒体与市场的关系在企业层面比聚合层面更加细微复杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融新闻究竟是影响股价还是仅反映已被市场吸收的信息，这一财务经济学中的开放问题在疫情大规模同时冲击新闻业与金融市场的背景下值得重新审视。已有研究多依赖聚合情绪指标，无法回答企业在个体层面的关系是否成立。

Method: 使用628万条新闻标题覆盖26家美国大型企业（2015-2025），过滤保留与特定企业实质性相关的报道，构建每日媒体立场（stance）指标，利用面板回归和含数据驱动结构断点的向量自回归（VAR）模型检验2020年疫情冲击前后媒体立场与股票收益率关系的结构变化。

Result: 研究发现，整体上鲜有证据表明疫情后发生了持续的媒体立场或股票收益率的市场级变化；但对部分企业而言，在其自身的结构断点附近出现了显著的动态关系。媒体与市场的相互作用在企业层面呈现出比聚合分析更为细致复杂的模式。

Conclusion: 理解媒体与市场互动需要企业层面的细分分析；该研究为考察市场条件变化下新闻与价格的交互作用提供了分析框架。

Abstract: Whether financial news influences stock prices or simply reflects information already incorporated into them remains an open question in financial economics. The COVID-19 pandemic provides an opportunity to revisit this question, as it disrupted both news coverage and financial markets on an unprecedented scale. Existing studies have largely approached the problem through aggregate sentiment measures, leaving it unclear whether the observed relationships also hold at the level of individual firms. We study this question using 6.28 million news headlines covering 26 large United States firms between 2015 and 2025. After filtering the corpus to retain materially relevant firm-specific coverage, we construct daily stance measures and examine how their relationship with stock returns changed around the 2020 shock using panel regressions and vector autoregressions with data-driven structural breaks. Our findings indicate that the relationship between financial news and equity markets is more nuanced than aggregate analyses alone suggest. While we find little evidence of a persistent market-wide change in media stance or stock returns following the pandemic, dynamic relationships emerge for a subset of firms around their own structural breaks. Taken together, these results suggest that understanding media-market interactions requires firm specific analysis and provide a framework for studying how news and prices interact under changing market conditions.

</details>


### [269] [Local-Global Feature Mixer and Trend-Guided Consistent Learning for Remaining Useful Life Prediction of Rotating Machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05925)
*Hanbyeol Park,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于剩余使用寿命预测的局部-全局特征混合器（LGFM）和趋势引导的滚动一致性损失（TG-RC），通过结合局部变化与全局退化状态、减少训练与递归推理之间的差异，提升了长期健康指标外推的稳定性和RUL预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统退化过程（DP）建模依赖于预设的固定退化函数，而神经网络虽能灵活表征复杂退化轨迹，但递归健康指标（HI）预测存在误差累积问题，且难以在长期预测中保持不可逆的退化趋势。现有方法在长期外推稳定性和预测精度上存在不足。

Method: 提出LGFM和TG-RC两个核心组件：LGFM将原始HI序列与统计特征和退化相关特征融合，降低对高频噪声的敏感性，同时捕捉局部变化和全局退化状态；TG-RC损失在传统单步均方误差基础上，补充了递归多步滚动损失和基于全局趋势先验的软动态时间规整（soft-DTW）对齐项，以缩小训练与递归推断之间的分布差异，并引导预测轨迹沿一致退化方向推进。

Result: 在两个公开轴承数据集上的实验表明，该框架在不同检测时间下均提升了长期HI外推的稳定性和RUL预测性能。LGFM保持较低计算复杂度，且TG-RC损失可灵活嵌入各类DP模型以改善其长期预测能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的LGFM与TG-RC相结合，有效缓解了递归预测中的误差累积和趋势漂移问题，在提升长期退化预测稳定性方面具有良好效果；TG-RC损失的通用性使其可作为即插即用模块增强现有DP模型的长期预测性能。

Abstract: Degradation process (DP) modeling is widely used for remaining useful life (RUL) prediction, particularly when run-to-failure data are limited. Neural networks can present complex degradation trajectories without prescribing a fixed degradation function; however, recursive health indicator (HI) forecasting is prone to error accumulation and may fail to preserve the irreversible degradation trend over long horizons. To address these limitations, this study proposes a local-global feature mixer (LGFM) and trend-guided rollout-consistent (TG-RC) loss. The LGFM combines the original HI sequence with statistical and degradation-related features to reduce sensitivity to high-frequency noise and capture both local changes and global degradation states. The TG-RC loss supplements the conventional one-step mean squared error with a recursive multi-step rollout loss and a soft dynamic time warping alignment term based on a global trend prior. Consequently, it reduces the discrepancy between training and recursive inference, while guiding the predicted trajectory toward a consistent degradation direction. Experiments on two public bearing datasets show that the proposed framework improves long-term HI extrapolation stability and RUL prediction performance across different inspection times. The LGFM also maintains low computational complexity, while the TG-RC loss can be incorporated into various DP models to improve their long-term forecasting performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [GenGA: Editable and Data-Grounded Graphical Abstract Generation for Academic Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05478)
*Takuro Kawada,Shunsuke Kitada,Hitoshi Iyatomi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenGA框架，直接从论文内容生成可编辑的矢量格式图形摘要（GA），解决了传统方法生成栅格图像难以后期编辑的问题，并引入结构独立性系数（SIC）来量化图形的编辑简易性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动生成科学图形的方法大多输出栅格格式（raster graphics），导致文字修改、布局调整等后期编辑极为困难，无法满足论文写作和同行评审中迭代修改的实际需求。

Method: GenGA框架将图形摘要生成定义为可编辑矢量图形的生成问题：通过将图形表示为具有层次结构的矢量元素集合，生成结果可直接导入现有绘图工具进行元素级编辑。同时提出结构独立性系数（SIC）作为新指标，基于局部修改传播到其他元素的程度来量化图形的编辑简易性。

Result: 实验结果表明，GenGA在编辑简易性上优于传统方法，在简洁性和语义对齐方面甚至超过人工绘制的图形摘要；SIC被验证为与人工编辑成本相关的有效指标。

Conclusion: 该研究从根本上将图形摘要生成重新定义为可编辑的矢量图形生成问题，紧密结合研究者的实际工作流程，显著促进了有效的科学交流。

Abstract: Graphical Abstracts (GAs) visually summarize the key findings of academic papers, playing a crucial role in facilitating the understanding of research content. Recently, advancements in vision-language models and image generation models have enabled the automatic generation of scientific figures based on paper content. However, most conventional methods output the generated results as raster graphics, making post-editing (e.g., text modification and layout changes) highly difficult. This poses a significant challenge, as they are unsuitable for the iterative figure revision process inherent in paper writing and peer review. To tackle these challenges, we define the novel task of generating editable GAs from paper content and propose GenGA, a new GA generation framework that directly produces figures in vector format. By generating figures as a collection of vector elements with a hierarchical structure, GenGA produces outputs that can be seamlessly imported into existing drawing tools for intuitive, element-level editing. Furthermore, we introduce the Structural Independence Coefficient (SIC), a metric that quantifies the editing simplicity of a figure based on the degree to which local modifications propagate to other elements. Experimental results show that GenGA achieves superior editing simplicity compared to conventional methods, and even surpasses human-authored GAs in conciseness and semantic alignment. We also validate SIC as an effective metric correlated with manual editing costs. This study fundamentally redefines GA generation as an editable vector graphic generation problem grounded in the practical workflows of researchers, significantly promoting effective scientific communication.

</details>


### [271] [ESVR: 3D Ellipsoid-based Sparse Volume Rendering via Structure-aware Primitive Learning and Per-primitive Ray Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05564)
*Suemin Jeon,Youjin Kim,Jungwoo Park,Kyungryun Lee,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出ESVR框架，一种基于椭球体稀疏体渲染方法，直接在大规模稀疏体数据中学习和渲染，实现高达四个数量级的压缩和43-223 FPS的实时渲染性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稀疏体数据的表示和渲染仍具挑战性。现有3DGS方法从DVR渲染图像而非原始体素学习，导致信息丢失，并限制了交互式探索中灵活传递函数控制的自由度。

Method: ESVR结合了三类核心组件：有界支撑的可微椭球体基元、结构感知基元学习与互补剪枝策略、逐基元光线采样策略以实现快速准确的传递函数映射。此外，引入基于块的优化方案，配合幽灵椭球体（ghost ellipsoids）在训练时提供边界上下文。
